local function scan(parent)
    for _, item in ipairs(parent:GetDescendants()) do
        -- We look for Scripts and ModuleScripts
        if item:IsA("LuaSourceContainer") then 
            print("------------------------------------------")
            print("PATH: " .. item:GetFullName())
            print("TYPE: " .. item.ClassName)
            print("------------------------------------------")
            print(item.Source)
            print("\n-- END OF SCRIPT --\n")
        end
    end
end

-- Existance in this list signifies that it is an emote, the value indicates if it is a looping emote
local emoteNames = { wave = false, point = false, dance1 = true, dance2 = true, dance3 = true, laugh = false, cheer = false}

function configureAnimationSet(name, fileList)
	if (animTable[name] ~= nil) then
		for _, connection in pairs(animTable[name].connections) do
			connection:disconnect()
		end
	end
	animTable[name] = {}
	animTable[name].count = 0
	animTable[name].totalWeight = 0	
	animTable[name].connections = {}

	-- check for config values
	local config = script:FindFirstChild(name)
	if (config ~= nil) then
--		print("Loading anims " .. name)
		table.insert(animTable[name].connections, config.ChildAdded:connect(function(child) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
		table.insert(animTable[name].connections, config.ChildRemoved:connect(function(child) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
		local idx = 1
		for _, childPart in pairs(config:GetChildren()) do
			if (childPart:IsA("Animation")) then
				table.insert(animTable[name].connections, childPart.Changed:connect(function(property) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
				animTable[name][idx] = {}
				animTable[name][idx].anim = childPart
				local weightObject = childPart:FindFirstChild("Weight")
				if (weightObject == nil) then
					animTable[name][idx].weight = 1
				else
					animTable[name][idx].weight = weightObject.Value
				end
				animTable[name].count = animTable[name].count + 1
				animTable[name].totalWeight = animTable[name].totalWeight + animTable[name][idx].weight
	--			print(name .. " [" .. idx .. "] " .. animTable[name][idx].anim.AnimationId .. " (" .. animTable[name][idx].weight .. ")")
				idx = idx + 1
			end
		end
	end

	-- fallback to defaults
	if (animTable[name].count <= 0) then
		for idx, anim in pairs(fileList) do
			animTable[name][idx] = {}
			animTable[name][idx].anim = Instance.new("Animation")
			animTable[name][idx].anim.Name = name
			animTable[name][idx].anim.AnimationId = anim.id
			animTable[name][idx].weight = anim.weight
			animTable[name].count = animTable[name].count + 1
			animTable[name].totalWeight = animTable[name].totalWeight + anim.weight
--			print(name .. " [" .. idx .. "] " .. anim.id .. " (" .. anim.weight .. ")")
		end
	end
end

-- Setup animation objects
function scriptChildModified(child)
	local fileList = animNames[child.Name]
	if (fileList ~= nil) then
		configureAnimationSet(child.Name, fileList)
	end	
end

script.ChildAdded:connect(scriptChildModified)
script.ChildRemoved:connect(scriptChildModified)

if animator then
	local animTracks = animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
	for i,track in ipairs(animTracks) do
		track:Stop(0)
		track:Destroy()
	end
end

	configureAnimationSet(name, fileList)
end	

-- ANIMATION

-- declarations
local toolAnim = "None"
local toolAnimTime = 0

local jumpAnimTime = 0
local jumpAnimDuration = 0.3

local toolTransitionTime = 0.1
local fallTransitionTime = 0.3
local jumpMaxLimbVelocity = 0.75

-- functions

function stopAllAnimations()
	local oldAnim = currentAnim

	-- return to idle if finishing an emote
	if (emoteNames[oldAnim] ~= nil and emoteNames[oldAnim] == false) then
		oldAnim = "idle"
	end

	currentAnim = ""
	currentAnimInstance = nil
	if (currentAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
		currentAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
	end

	if (currentAnimTrack ~= nil) then
		currentAnimTrack:Stop()
		currentAnimTrack:Destroy()
		currentAnimTrack = nil
	end
	return oldAnim
end

function setAnimationSpeed(speed)
	if speed ~= currentAnimSpeed then
		currentAnimSpeed = speed
		currentAnimTrack:AdjustSpeed(currentAnimSpeed)
	end
end

function keyFrameReachedFunc(frameName)
	if (frameName == "End") then

		local repeatAnim = currentAnim
		-- return to idle if finishing an emote
		if (emoteNames[repeatAnim] ~= nil and emoteNames[repeatAnim] == false) then
			repeatAnim = "idle"
		end
		
		local animSpeed = currentAnimSpeed
		playAnimation(repeatAnim, 0.0, Humanoid)
		setAnimationSpeed(animSpeed)
	end
end

-- Preload animations
function playAnimation(animName, transitionTime, humanoid) 
		
	local roll = math.random(1, animTable[animName].totalWeight) 
	local origRoll = roll
	local idx = 1
	while (roll > animTable[animName][idx].weight) do
		roll = roll - animTable[animName][idx].weight
		idx = idx + 1
	end
--		print(animName .. " " .. idx .. " [" .. origRoll .. "]")
	local anim = animTable[animName][idx].anim

	-- switch animation		
	if (anim ~= currentAnimInstance) then
		
		if (currentAnimTrack ~= nil) then
			currentAnimTrack:Stop(transitionTime)
			currentAnimTrack:Destroy()
		end

		currentAnimSpeed = 1.0
	
		-- load it to the humanoid; get AnimationTrack
		currentAnimTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim)
		currentAnimTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
		 
		-- play the animation
		currentAnimTrack:Play(transitionTime)
		currentAnim = animName
		currentAnimInstance = anim

		-- set up keyframe name triggers
		if (currentAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
			currentAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
		end
		currentAnimKeyframeHandler = currentAnimTrack.KeyframeReached:connect(keyFrameReachedFunc)
		
	end

end

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

function onRunning(speed)
	if speed > 3 and speed < 18 then
		playAnimation("walk", 0.1, Humanoid)
		setAnimationSpeed(speed / 14.5)
		pose = "Walking"
	elseif speed >= 18 then
		--playAnimation("run", 0.3, Humanoid)
		--setAnimationSpeed(speed / 19.5)
		pose = "Walking"
	else
		if emoteNames[currentAnim] == nil then
			playAnimation("idle", 0.1, Humanoid)
			pose = "Standing"
		end
	end
end

function onDied()
	pose = "Dead"
end

function onJumping()
	playAnimation("jump", 0.1, Humanoid)
	jumpAnimTime = jumpAnimDuration
	pose = "Jumping"
end

function onClimbing(speed)
	playAnimation("climb", 0.1, Humanoid)
	setAnimationSpeed(speed / 12.0)
	pose = "Climbing"
end

function onGettingUp()
	pose = "GettingUp"
end

function onFreeFall()
	if (jumpAnimTime <= 0) then
		playAnimation("fall", fallTransitionTime, Humanoid)
	end
	pose = "FreeFall"
end

function onFallingDown()
	pose = "FallingDown"
end

function onSeated()
	pose = "Seated"
end

function onPlatformStanding()
	pose = "PlatformStanding"
end

function onSwimming(speed)
	if speed > 0 then
		pose = "Running"
	else
		pose = "Standing"
	end
end

function moveSit()
	RightShoulder.MaxVelocity = 0.15
	LeftShoulder.MaxVelocity = 0.15
	RightShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(3.14 /2)
	LeftShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(-3.14 /2)
	RightHip:SetDesiredAngle(3.14 /2)
	LeftHip:SetDesiredAngle(-3.14 /2)
end

local lastTick = 0

function move(time)
	local amplitude = 1
	local frequency = 1
  	local deltaTime = time - lastTick
  	lastTick = time

	local climbFudge = 0
	local setAngles = false

  	if (jumpAnimTime > 0) then
  		jumpAnimTime = jumpAnimTime - deltaTime
  	end

	if (pose == "FreeFall" and jumpAnimTime <= 0) then
		playAnimation("fall", fallTransitionTime, Humanoid)
	elseif (pose == "Seated") then
		playAnimation("sit", 0.5, Humanoid)
		return
	elseif (pose == "Walking") then
		playAnimation("walk", 0.1, Humanoid)
	elseif (pose == "Running") then
		--playAnimation("run", 0.1, Humanoid)
	elseif (pose == "Dead" or pose == "GettingUp" or pose == "FallingDown" or pose == "Seated" or pose == "PlatformStanding") then
--		print("Wha " .. pose)
		stopAllAnimations()
		amplitude = 0.1
		frequency = 1
		setAngles = true
	end

	if (setAngles) then
		local desiredAngle = amplitude * math.sin(time * frequency)

		RightShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(desiredAngle + climbFudge)
		LeftShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(desiredAngle - climbFudge)
		RightHip:SetDesiredAngle(-desiredAngle)
		LeftHip:SetDesiredAngle(-desiredAngle)
	end
end

-- connect events
Humanoid.Died:connect(onDied)
Humanoid.Running:connect(onRunning)
Humanoid.Jumping:connect(onJumping)
Humanoid.Climbing:connect(onClimbing)
Humanoid.GettingUp:connect(onGettingUp)
Humanoid.FreeFalling:connect(onFreeFall)
Humanoid.FallingDown:connect(onFallingDown)
Humanoid.Seated:connect(onSeated)
Humanoid.PlatformStanding:connect(onPlatformStanding)
Humanoid.Swimming:connect(onSwimming)

---- setup emote chat hook
game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Chatted:connect(function(msg)
	local emote = ""
	if msg == "/e dance" then
		emote = dances[math.random(1, #dances)]
	elseif (string.sub(msg, 1, 3) == "/e ") then
		emote = string.sub(msg, 4)
	elseif (string.sub(msg, 1, 7) == "/emote ") then
		emote = string.sub(msg, 8)
	end
	
	if (pose == "Standing" and emoteNames[emote] ~= nil) then
		playAnimation(emote, 0.1, Humanoid)
	end

end)

-- emote bindable hook
script:WaitForChild("PlayEmote").OnInvoke = function(emote)
	-- Only play emotes when idling
	if pose ~= "Standing" then
		return
	end
	if emoteNames[emote] ~= nil then
		-- Default emotes
		playAnimation(emote, EMOTE_TRANSITION_TIME, Humanoid)

		if userPlayEmoteByIdAnimTrackReturn then
			return true, currentAnimTrack
		else
			return true
		end
	end

	-- Return false to indicate that the emote could not be played
	return false
end
-- main program

-- initialize to idle
playAnimation("idle", 0.1, Humanoid)
pose = "Standing"

while Figure.Parent ~= nil do
	local _, time = wait(0.1)
	move(time)
end

  03:10:35.968  -- Created by Quenty (@Quenty, follow me on twitter).
-- Should work with only ONE copy, seamlessly with weapons, trains, et cetera.
-- Parts should be ANCHORED before use. It will, however, store relatives values and so when tools are reparented, it'll fix them.

--[[ INSTRUCTIONS
- Place in the model
- Make sure model is anchored
- That's it. It will weld the model and all children. 

THIS SCRIPT SHOULD BE USED ONLY BY ITSELF. THE MODEL SHOULD BE ANCHORED. 
THIS SCRIPT SHOULD BE USED ONLY BY ITSELF. THE MODEL SHOULD BE ANCHORED. 
THIS SCRIPT SHOULD BE USED ONLY BY ITSELF. THE MODEL SHOULD BE ANCHORED. 
THIS SCRIPT SHOULD BE USED ONLY BY ITSELF. THE MODEL SHOULD BE ANCHORED. 
THIS SCRIPT SHOULD BE USED ONLY BY ITSELF. THE MODEL SHOULD BE ANCHORED. 
THIS SCRIPT SHOULD BE USED ONLY BY ITSELF. THE MODEL SHOULD BE ANCHORED. 
THIS SCRIPT SHOULD BE USED ONLY BY ITSELF. THE MODEL SHOULD BE ANCHORED. 
THIS SCRIPT SHOULD BE USED ONLY BY ITSELF. THE MODEL SHOULD BE ANCHORED. 

This script is designed to be used is a regular script. In a local script it will weld, but it will not attempt to handle ancestory changes. 
]]

--[[ DOCUMENTATION
- Will work in tools. If ran more than once it will not create more than one weld.  This is especially useful for tools that are dropped and then picked up again.
- Will work in PBS servers
- Will work as long as it starts out with the part anchored
- Stores the relative CFrame as a CFrame value
- Takes careful measure to reduce lag by not having a joint set off or affected by the parts offset from origin
- Utilizes a recursive algorith to find all parts in the model
- Will reweld on script reparent if the script is initially parented to a tool.
- Welds as fast as possible
]]

-- qPerfectionWeld.lua
-- Created 10/6/2014
-- Author: Quenty
-- Version 1.0.3

-- Updated 10/14/2014 - Updated to 1.0.1
--- Bug fix with existing ROBLOX welds ? Repro by asimo3089

-- Updated 10/14/2014 - Updated to 1.0.2
--- Fixed bug fix. 

-- Updated 10/14/2014 - Updated to 1.0.3
--- Now handles joints semi-acceptably. May be rather hacky with some joints. :/

local NEVER_BREAK_JOINTS = false -- If you set this to true it will never break joints (this can create some welding issues, but can save stuff like hinges).


local function CallOnChildren(Instance, FunctionToCall)
	-- Calls a function on each of the children of a certain object, using recursion.  

	FunctionToCall(Instance)

	for _, Child in next, Instance:GetChildren() do
		CallOnChildren(Child, FunctionToCall)
	end
end

local function GetNearestParent(Instance, ClassName)
	-- Returns the nearest parent of a certain class, or returns nil

	local Ancestor = Instance
	repeat
		Ancestor = Ancestor.Parent
		if Ancestor == nil then
			return nil
		end
	until Ancestor:IsA(ClassName)

	return Ancestor
end

local function GetBricks(StartInstance)
	local List = {}

	-- if StartInstance:IsA("BasePart") then
	-- 	List[#List+1] = StartInstance
	-- end

	CallOnChildren(StartInstance, function(Item)
		if Item:IsA("BasePart") then
			List[#List+1] = Item;
		end
	end)

	return List
end

local function Modify(Instance, Values)
	-- Modifies an Instance by using a table.  

	assert(type(Values) == "table", "Values is not a table");

	for Index, Value in next, Values do
		if type(Index) == "number" then
			Value.Parent = Instance
		else
			Instance[Index] = Value
		end
	end
	return Instance
end

local function Make(ClassType, Properties)
	-- Using a syntax hack to create a nice way to Make new items.  

	return Modify(Instance.new(ClassType), Properties)
end

local Surfaces = {"TopSurface", "BottomSurface", "LeftSurface", "RightSurface", "FrontSurface", "BackSurface"}
local HingSurfaces = {"Hinge", "Motor", "SteppingMotor"}

local function HasWheelJoint(Part)
	for _, SurfaceName in pairs(Surfaces) do
		for _, HingSurfaceName in pairs(HingSurfaces) do
			if Part[SurfaceName].Name == HingSurfaceName then
				return true
			end
		end
	end
	
	return false
end

local function ShouldBreakJoints(Part)
	--- We do not want to break joints of wheels/hinges. This takes the utmost care to not do this. There are
	--  definitely some edge cases. 

	if NEVER_BREAK_JOINTS then
		return false
	end
	
	if HasWheelJoint(Part) then
		return false
	end
	
	local Connected = Part:GetConnectedParts()
	
	if #Connected == 1 then
		return false
	end
	
	for _, Item in pairs(Connected) do
		if HasWheelJoint(Item) then
			return false
		elseif not Item:IsDescendantOf(script.Parent) then
			return false
		end
	end
	
	return true
end

local function WeldTogether(Part0, Part1, JointType, WeldParent)
	--- Weld's 2 parts together
	-- @param Part0 The first part
	-- @param Part1 The second part (Dependent part most of the time).
	-- @param [JointType] The type of joint. Defaults to weld.
	-- @param [WeldParent] Parent of the weld, Defaults to Part0 (so GC is better).
	-- @return The weld created.

	JointType = JointType or "Weld"
	local RelativeValue = Part1:FindFirstChild("qRelativeCFrameWeldValue")
	
	local NewWeld = Part1:FindFirstChild("qCFrameWeldThingy") or Instance.new(JointType)
	Modify(NewWeld, {
		Name = "qCFrameWeldThingy";
		Part0  = Part0;
		Part1  = Part1;
		C0     = CFrame.new();--Part0.CFrame:inverse();
		C1     = RelativeValue and RelativeValue.Value or Part1.CFrame:toObjectSpace(Part0.CFrame); --Part1.CFrame:inverse() * Part0.CFrame;-- Part1.CFrame:inverse();
		Parent = Part1;
	})

	if not RelativeValue then
		RelativeValue = Make("CFrameValue", {
			Parent     = Part1;
			Name       = "qRelativeCFrameWeldValue";
			Archivable = true;
			Value      = NewWeld.C1;
		})
	end

	return NewWeld
end

local function WeldParts(Parts, MainPart, JointType, DoNotUnanchor)
	-- @param Parts The Parts to weld. Should be anchored to prevent really horrible results.
	-- @param MainPart The part to weld the model to (can be in the model).
	-- @param [JointType] The type of joint. Defaults to weld. 
	-- @parm DoNotUnanchor Boolean, if true, will not unachor the model after cmopletion.
	
	for _, Part in pairs(Parts) do
		if ShouldBreakJoints(Part) then
			Part:BreakJoints()
		end
	end
	
	for _, Part in pairs(Parts) do
		if Part ~= MainPart then
			WeldTogether(MainPart, Part, JointType, MainPart)
		end
	end

	if not DoNotUnanchor then
		for _, Part in pairs(Parts) do
			Part.Anchored = false
		end
		MainPart.Anchored = false
	end
end

local function PerfectionWeld()	
	local Tool = GetNearestParent(script, "Tool")

	local Parts = GetBricks(script.Parent)
	local PrimaryPart = Tool and Tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") and Tool.Handle:IsA("BasePart") and Tool.Handle or script.Parent:IsA("Model") and script.Parent.PrimaryPart or Parts[1]

	if PrimaryPart then
		WeldParts(Parts, PrimaryPart, "Weld", false)
	else
		warn("qWeld - Unable to weld part")
	end
	
	return Tool
end

local Tool = PerfectionWeld()


if Tool and script.ClassName == "Script" then
	--- Don't bother with local scripts

	script.Parent.AncestryChanged:connect(function()
		PerfectionWeld()
	end)
end

local dances = {"dance1", "dance2", "dance3"}

-- Existance in this list signifies that it is an emote, the value indicates if it is a looping emote
local emoteNames = { wave = false, point = false, dance1 = true, dance2 = true, dance3 = true, laugh = false, cheer = false}

function configureAnimationSet(name, fileList)
	if (animTable[name] ~= nil) then
		for _, connection in pairs(animTable[name].connections) do
			connection:disconnect()
		end
	end
	animTable[name] = {}
	animTable[name].count = 0
	animTable[name].totalWeight = 0	
	animTable[name].connections = {}

	-- check for config values
	local config = script:FindFirstChild(name)
	if (config ~= nil) then
--		print("Loading anims " .. name)
		table.insert(animTable[name].connections, config.ChildAdded:connect(function(child) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
		table.insert(animTable[name].connections, config.ChildRemoved:connect(function(child) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
		local idx = 1
		for _, childPart in pairs(config:GetChildren()) do
			if (childPart:IsA("Animation")) then
				table.insert(animTable[name].connections, childPart.Changed:connect(function(property) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
				animTable[name][idx] = {}
				animTable[name][idx].anim = childPart
				local weightObject = childPart:FindFirstChild("Weight")
				if (weightObject == nil) then
					animTable[name][idx].weight = 1
				else
					animTable[name][idx].weight = weightObject.Value
				end
				animTable[name].count = animTable[name].count + 1
				animTable[name].totalWeight = animTable[name].totalWeight + animTable[name][idx].weight
	--			print(name .. " [" .. idx .. "] " .. animTable[name][idx].anim.AnimationId .. " (" .. animTable[name][idx].weight .. ")")
				idx = idx + 1
			end
		end
	end

	-- fallback to defaults
	if (animTable[name].count <= 0) then
		for idx, anim in pairs(fileList) do
			animTable[name][idx] = {}
			animTable[name][idx].anim = Instance.new("Animation")
			animTable[name][idx].anim.Name = name
			animTable[name][idx].anim.AnimationId = anim.id
			animTable[name][idx].weight = anim.weight
			animTable[name].count = animTable[name].count + 1
			animTable[name].totalWeight = animTable[name].totalWeight + anim.weight
--			print(name .. " [" .. idx .. "] " .. anim.id .. " (" .. anim.weight .. ")")
		end
	end
end

-- Setup animation objects
function scriptChildModified(child)
	local fileList = animNames[child.Name]
	if (fileList ~= nil) then
		configureAnimationSet(child.Name, fileList)
	end	
end

script.ChildAdded:connect(scriptChildModified)
script.ChildRemoved:connect(scriptChildModified)

-- Clear any existing animation tracks
-- Fixes issue with characters that are moved in and out of the Workspace accumulating tracks
local animator = if Humanoid then Humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") else nil
if animator then
	local animTracks = animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
	for i,track in ipairs(animTracks) do
		track:Stop(0)
		track:Destroy()
	end
end


for name, fileList in pairs(animNames) do 
	configureAnimationSet(name, fileList)
end	

-- ANIMATION

-- declarations
local toolAnim = "None"
local toolAnimTime = 0

local jumpAnimTime = 0
local jumpAnimDuration = 0.3

local toolTransitionTime = 0.1
local fallTransitionTime = 0.3
local jumpMaxLimbVelocity = 0.75

-- functions

function stopAllAnimations()
	local oldAnim = currentAnim

	-- return to idle if finishing an emote
	if (emoteNames[oldAnim] ~= nil and emoteNames[oldAnim] == false) then
		oldAnim = "idle"
	end

	currentAnim = ""
	currentAnimInstance = nil
	if (currentAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
		currentAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
	end

	if (currentAnimTrack ~= nil) then
		currentAnimTrack:Stop()
		currentAnimTrack:Destroy()
		currentAnimTrack = nil
	end
	return oldAnim
end

function setAnimationSpeed(speed)
	if speed ~= currentAnimSpeed then
		currentAnimSpeed = speed
		currentAnimTrack:AdjustSpeed(currentAnimSpeed)
	end
end

function keyFrameReachedFunc(frameName)
	if (frameName == "End") then

		local repeatAnim = currentAnim
		-- return to idle if finishing an emote
		if (emoteNames[repeatAnim] ~= nil and emoteNames[repeatAnim] == false) then
			repeatAnim = "idle"
		end
		
		local animSpeed = currentAnimSpeed
		playAnimation(repeatAnim, 0.0, Humanoid)
		setAnimationSpeed(animSpeed)
	end
end

-- Preload animations
function playAnimation(animName, transitionTime, humanoid) 
		
	local roll = math.random(1, animTable[animName].totalWeight) 
	local origRoll = roll
	local idx = 1
	while (roll > animTable[animName][idx].weight) do
		roll = roll - animTable[animName][idx].weight
		idx = idx + 1
	end
--		print(animName .. " " .. idx .. " [" .. origRoll .. "]")
	local anim = animTable[animName][idx].anim

	-- switch animation		
	if (anim ~= currentAnimInstance) then
		
		if (currentAnimTrack ~= nil) then
			currentAnimTrack:Stop(transitionTime)
			currentAnimTrack:Destroy()
		end

		currentAnimSpeed = 1.0
	
		-- load it to the humanoid; get AnimationTrack
		currentAnimTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim)
		currentAnimTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
		 
		-- play the animation
		currentAnimTrack:Play(transitionTime)
		currentAnim = animName
		currentAnimInstance = anim

		-- set up keyframe name triggers
		if (currentAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
			currentAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
		end
		currentAnimKeyframeHandler = currentAnimTrack.KeyframeReached:connect(keyFrameReachedFunc)
		
	end

end

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

local toolAnimName = ""
local toolAnimTrack = nil
local toolAnimInstance = nil
local currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler = nil

function toolKeyFrameReachedFunc(frameName)
	if (frameName == "End") then
--		print("Keyframe : ".. frameName)	
		playToolAnimation(toolAnimName, 0.0, Humanoid)
	end
end


function playToolAnimation(animName, transitionTime, humanoid, priority)	 
		
		local roll = math.random(1, animTable[animName].totalWeight) 
		local origRoll = roll
		local idx = 1
		while (roll > animTable[animName][idx].weight) do
			roll = roll - animTable[animName][idx].weight
			idx = idx + 1
		end
--		print(animName .. " * " .. idx .. " [" .. origRoll .. "]")
		local anim = animTable[animName][idx].anim

		if (toolAnimInstance ~= anim) then
			
			if (toolAnimTrack ~= nil) then
				toolAnimTrack:Stop()
				toolAnimTrack:Destroy()
				transitionTime = 0
			end
					
			-- load it to the humanoid; get AnimationTrack
			toolAnimTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim)
			if priority then
				toolAnimTrack.Priority = priority
			end
			 
			-- play the animation
			toolAnimTrack:Play(transitionTime)
			toolAnimName = animName
			toolAnimInstance = anim

			currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler = toolAnimTrack.KeyframeReached:connect(toolKeyFrameReachedFunc)
		end
end

function stopToolAnimations()
	local oldAnim = toolAnimName

	if (currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
		currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
	end

	toolAnimName = ""
	toolAnimInstance = nil
	if (toolAnimTrack ~= nil) then
		toolAnimTrack:Stop()
		toolAnimTrack:Destroy()
		toolAnimTrack = nil
	end


	return oldAnim
end

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


function onRunning(speed)
	if speed > 0.01 then
		playAnimation("walk", 0.1, Humanoid)
		if currentAnimInstance and currentAnimInstance.AnimationId == "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=180426354" then
			setAnimationSpeed(speed / 14.5)
		end
		pose = "Running"
	else
		if emoteNames[currentAnim] == nil then
			playAnimation("idle", 0.1, Humanoid)
			pose = "Standing"
		end
	end
end

function onDied()
	pose = "Dead"
end

function onJumping()
	playAnimation("jump", 0.1, Humanoid)
	jumpAnimTime = jumpAnimDuration
	pose = "Jumping"
end

function onClimbing(speed)
	playAnimation("climb", 0.1, Humanoid)
	setAnimationSpeed(speed / 12.0)
	pose = "Climbing"
end

function onGettingUp()
	pose = "GettingUp"
end

function onFreeFall()
	if (jumpAnimTime <= 0) then
		playAnimation("fall", fallTransitionTime, Humanoid)
	end
	pose = "FreeFall"
end

function onFallingDown()
	pose = "FallingDown"
end

function onSeated()
	pose = "Seated"
end

function onPlatformStanding()
	pose = "PlatformStanding"
end

function onSwimming(speed)
	if speed > 0 then
		pose = "Running"
	else
		pose = "Standing"
	end
end

function getTool()	
	for _, kid in ipairs(Figure:GetChildren()) do
		if kid.className == "Tool" then return kid end
	end
	return nil
end

function getToolAnim(tool)
	for _, c in ipairs(tool:GetChildren()) do
		if c.Name == "toolanim" and c.className == "StringValue" then
			return c
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function animateTool()
	
	if (toolAnim == "None") then
		playToolAnimation("toolnone", toolTransitionTime, Humanoid, Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle)
		return
	end

	if (toolAnim == "Slash") then
		playToolAnimation("toolslash", 0, Humanoid, Enum.AnimationPriority.Action)
		return
	end

	if (toolAnim == "Lunge") then
		playToolAnimation("toollunge", 0, Humanoid, Enum.AnimationPriority.Action)
		return
	end
end

function moveSit()
	RightShoulder.MaxVelocity = 0.15
	LeftShoulder.MaxVelocity = 0.15
	RightShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(3.14 /2)
	LeftShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(-3.14 /2)
	RightHip:SetDesiredAngle(3.14 /2)
	LeftHip:SetDesiredAngle(-3.14 /2)
end

local lastTick = 0

function move(time)
	local amplitude = 1
	local frequency = 1
  	local deltaTime = time - lastTick
  	lastTick = time

	local climbFudge = 0
	local setAngles = false

  	if (jumpAnimTime > 0) then
  		jumpAnimTime = jumpAnimTime - deltaTime
  	end

	if (pose == "FreeFall" and jumpAnimTime <= 0) then
		playAnimation("fall", fallTransitionTime, Humanoid)
	elseif (pose == "Seated") then
		playAnimation("sit", 0.5, Humanoid)
		return
	elseif (pose == "Running") then
		playAnimation("walk", 0.1, Humanoid)
	elseif (pose == "Dead" or pose == "GettingUp" or pose == "FallingDown" or pose == "Seated" or pose == "PlatformStanding") then
--		print("Wha " .. pose)
		stopAllAnimations()
		amplitude = 0.1
		frequency = 1
		setAngles = true
	end

	if (setAngles) then
		local desiredAngle = amplitude * math.sin(time * frequency)

		RightShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(desiredAngle + climbFudge)
		LeftShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(desiredAngle - climbFudge)
		RightHip:SetDesiredAngle(-desiredAngle)
		LeftHip:SetDesiredAngle(-desiredAngle)
	end

	-- Tool Animation handling
	local tool = getTool()
	if tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
	
		local animStringValueObject = getToolAnim(tool)

		if animStringValueObject then
			toolAnim = animStringValueObject.Value
			-- message recieved, delete StringValue
			animStringValueObject.Parent = nil
			toolAnimTime = time + .3
		end

		if time > toolAnimTime then
			toolAnimTime = 0
			toolAnim = "None"
		end

		animateTool()		
	else
		stopToolAnimations()
		toolAnim = "None"
		toolAnimInstance = nil
		toolAnimTime = 0
	end
end

-- connect events
Humanoid.Died:connect(onDied)
Humanoid.Running:connect(onRunning)
Humanoid.Jumping:connect(onJumping)
Humanoid.Climbing:connect(onClimbing)
Humanoid.GettingUp:connect(onGettingUp)
Humanoid.FreeFalling:connect(onFreeFall)
Humanoid.FallingDown:connect(onFallingDown)
Humanoid.Seated:connect(onSeated)
Humanoid.PlatformStanding:connect(onPlatformStanding)
Humanoid.Swimming:connect(onSwimming)

---- setup emote chat hook
game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Chatted:connect(function(msg)
	local emote = ""
	if msg == "/e dance" then
		emote = dances[math.random(1, #dances)]
	elseif (string.sub(msg, 1, 3) == "/e ") then
		emote = string.sub(msg, 4)
	elseif (string.sub(msg, 1, 7) == "/emote ") then
		emote = string.sub(msg, 8)
	end
	
	if (pose == "Standing" and emoteNames[emote] ~= nil) then
		playAnimation(emote, 0.1, Humanoid)
	end

end)

-- emote bindable hook
script:WaitForChild("PlayEmote").OnInvoke = function(emote)
	-- Only play emotes when idling
	if pose ~= "Standing" then
		return
	end
	if emoteNames[emote] ~= nil then
		-- Default emotes
		playAnimation(emote, EMOTE_TRANSITION_TIME, Humanoid)

		return true, currentAnimTrack
	end

	-- Return false to indicate that the emote could not be played
	return false
end
-- main program

-- initialize to idle
playAnimation("idle", 0.1, Humanoid)
pose = "Standing"

while Figure.Parent ~= nil do
	local _, time = wait(0.1)
	move(time)
end

function configureAnimationSet(name, fileList)
	if (animTable[name] ~= nil) then
		for _, connection in pairs(animTable[name].connections) do
			connection:disconnect()
		end
	end
	animTable[name] = {}
	animTable[name].count = 0
	animTable[name].totalWeight = 0	
	animTable[name].connections = {}

	-- check for config values
	local config = script:FindFirstChild(name)
	if (config ~= nil) then
--		print("Loading anims " .. name)
		table.insert(animTable[name].connections, config.ChildAdded:connect(function(child) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
		table.insert(animTable[name].connections, config.ChildRemoved:connect(function(child) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
		local idx = 1
		for _, childPart in pairs(config:GetChildren()) do
			if (childPart:IsA("Animation")) then
				table.insert(animTable[name].connections, childPart.Changed:connect(function(property) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
				animTable[name][idx] = {}
				animTable[name][idx].anim = childPart
				local weightObject = childPart:FindFirstChild("Weight")
				if (weightObject == nil) then
					animTable[name][idx].weight = 1
				else
					animTable[name][idx].weight = weightObject.Value
				end
				animTable[name].count = animTable[name].count + 1
				animTable[name].totalWeight = animTable[name].totalWeight + animTable[name][idx].weight
	--			print(name .. " [" .. idx .. "] " .. animTable[name][idx].anim.AnimationId .. " (" .. animTable[name][idx].weight .. ")")
				idx = idx + 1
			end
		end
	end

	-- fallback to defaults
	if (animTable[name].count <= 0) then
		for idx, anim in pairs(fileList) do
			animTable[name][idx] = {}
			animTable[name][idx].anim = Instance.new("Animation")
			animTable[name][idx].anim.Name = name
			animTable[name][idx].anim.AnimationId = anim.id
			animTable[name][idx].weight = anim.weight
			animTable[name].count = animTable[name].count + 1
			animTable[name].totalWeight = animTable[name].totalWeight + anim.weight
--			print(name .. " [" .. idx .. "] " .. anim.id .. " (" .. anim.weight .. ")")
		end
	end
end

local jumpAnimDuration = 0.3

local toolTransitionTime = 0.1
local fallTransitionTime = 0.3
local jumpMaxLimbVelocity = 0.75

-- functions

function stopAllAnimations()
	local oldAnim = currentAnim

	-- return to idle if finishing an emote
	if (emoteNames[oldAnim] ~= nil and emoteNames[oldAnim] == false) then
		oldAnim = "idle"
	end

	currentAnim = ""
	currentAnimInstance = nil
	if (currentAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
		currentAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
	end

	if (currentAnimTrack ~= nil) then
		currentAnimTrack:Stop()
		currentAnimTrack:Destroy()
		currentAnimTrack = nil
	end
	return oldAnim
end

function setAnimationSpeed(speed)
	if speed ~= currentAnimSpeed then
		currentAnimSpeed = speed
		currentAnimTrack:AdjustSpeed(currentAnimSpeed)
	end
end

function keyFrameReachedFunc(frameName)
	if (frameName == "End") then

		local repeatAnim = currentAnim
		-- return to idle if finishing an emote
		if (emoteNames[repeatAnim] ~= nil and emoteNames[repeatAnim] == false) then
			repeatAnim = "idle"
		end
		
		local animSpeed = currentAnimSpeed
		playAnimation(repeatAnim, 0.0, Humanoid)
		setAnimationSpeed(animSpeed)
	end
end

-- Preload animations
function playAnimation(animName, transitionTime, humanoid) 
		
	local roll = math.random(1, animTable[animName].totalWeight) 
	local origRoll = roll
	local idx = 1
	while (roll > animTable[animName][idx].weight) do
		roll = roll - animTable[animName][idx].weight
		idx = idx + 1
	end
--		print(animName .. " " .. idx .. " [" .. origRoll .. "]")
	local anim = animTable[animName][idx].anim

	-- switch animation		
	if (anim ~= currentAnimInstance) then
		
		if (currentAnimTrack ~= nil) then
			currentAnimTrack:Stop(transitionTime)
			currentAnimTrack:Destroy()
		end

		currentAnimSpeed = 1.0
	
		-- load it to the humanoid; get AnimationTrack
		currentAnimTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim)
		currentAnimTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
		 
		-- play the animation
		currentAnimTrack:Play(transitionTime)
		currentAnim = animName
		currentAnimInstance = anim

		-- set up keyframe name triggers
		if (currentAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
			currentAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
		end
		currentAnimKeyframeHandler = currentAnimTrack.KeyframeReached:connect(keyFrameReachedFunc)
		
	end

end

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

local toolAnimName = ""
local toolAnimTrack = nil
local toolAnimInstance = nil
local currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler = nil

function toolKeyFrameReachedFunc(frameName)
	if (frameName == "End") then
--		print("Keyframe : ".. frameName)	
		playToolAnimation(toolAnimName, 0.0, Humanoid)
	end
end


function playToolAnimation(animName, transitionTime, humanoid, priority)	 
		
		local roll = math.random(1, animTable[animName].totalWeight) 
		local origRoll = roll
		local idx = 1
		while (roll > animTable[animName][idx].weight) do
			roll = roll - animTable[animName][idx].weight
			idx = idx + 1
		end
--		print(animName .. " * " .. idx .. " [" .. origRoll .. "]")
		local anim = animTable[animName][idx].anim

		if (toolAnimInstance ~= anim) then
			
			if (toolAnimTrack ~= nil) then
				toolAnimTrack:Stop()
				toolAnimTrack:Destroy()
				transitionTime = 0
			end
					
			-- load it to the humanoid; get AnimationTrack
			toolAnimTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim)
			if priority then
				toolAnimTrack.Priority = priority
			end
			 
			-- play the animation
			toolAnimTrack:Play(transitionTime)
			toolAnimName = animName
			toolAnimInstance = anim

			currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler = toolAnimTrack.KeyframeReached:connect(toolKeyFrameReachedFunc)
		end
end

function stopToolAnimations()
	local oldAnim = toolAnimName

	if (currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
		currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
	end

	toolAnimName = ""
	toolAnimInstance = nil
	if (toolAnimTrack ~= nil) then
		toolAnimTrack:Stop()
		toolAnimTrack:Destroy()
		toolAnimTrack = nil
	end


	return oldAnim
end

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


function onRunning(speed)
	if speed > 0.01 then
		playAnimation("walk", 0.1, Humanoid)
		if currentAnimInstance and currentAnimInstance.AnimationId == "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=180426354" then
			setAnimationSpeed(speed / 14.5)
		end
		pose = "Running"
	else
		if emoteNames[currentAnim] == nil then
			playAnimation("idle", 0.1, Humanoid)
			pose = "Standing"
		end
	end
end

function onDied()
	pose = "Dead"
end

function onJumping()
	playAnimation("jump", 0.1, Humanoid)
	jumpAnimTime = jumpAnimDuration
	pose = "Jumping"
end

function onClimbing(speed)
	playAnimation("climb", 0.1, Humanoid)
	setAnimationSpeed(speed / 12.0)
	pose = "Climbing"
end

function onGettingUp()
	pose = "GettingUp"
end

function onFreeFall()
	if (jumpAnimTime <= 0) then
		playAnimation("fall", fallTransitionTime, Humanoid)
	end
	pose = "FreeFall"
end

function onFallingDown()
	pose = "FallingDown"
end

function onSeated()
	pose = "Seated"
end

function onPlatformStanding()
	pose = "PlatformStanding"
end

function onSwimming(speed)
	if speed > 0 then
		pose = "Running"
	else
		pose = "Standing"
	end
end

function getTool()	
	for _, kid in ipairs(Figure:GetChildren()) do
		if kid.className == "Tool" then return kid end
	end
	return nil
end

function getToolAnim(tool)
	for _, c in ipairs(tool:GetChildren()) do
		if c.Name == "toolanim" and c.className == "StringValue" then
			return c
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function animateTool()
	
	if (toolAnim == "None") then
		playToolAnimation("toolnone", toolTransitionTime, Humanoid, Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle)
		return
	end

	if (toolAnim == "Slash") then
		playToolAnimation("toolslash", 0, Humanoid, Enum.AnimationPriority.Action)
		return
	end

	if (toolAnim == "Lunge") then
		playToolAnimation("toollunge", 0, Humanoid, Enum.AnimationPriority.Action)
		return
	end
end

function moveSit()
	RightShoulder.MaxVelocity = 0.15
	LeftShoulder.MaxVelocity = 0.15
	RightShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(3.14 /2)
	LeftShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(-3.14 /2)
	RightHip:SetDesiredAngle(3.14 /2)
	LeftHip:SetDesiredAngle(-3.14 /2)
end

local lastTick = 0

function move(time)
	local amplitude = 1
	local frequency = 1
  	local deltaTime = time - lastTick
  	lastTick = time

	local climbFudge = 0
	local setAngles = false

  	if (jumpAnimTime > 0) then
  		jumpAnimTime = jumpAnimTime - deltaTime
  	end

	if (pose == "FreeFall" and jumpAnimTime <= 0) then
		playAnimation("fall", fallTransitionTime, Humanoid)
	elseif (pose == "Seated") then
		playAnimation("sit", 0.5, Humanoid)
		return
	elseif (pose == "Running") then
		playAnimation("walk", 0.1, Humanoid)
	elseif (pose == "Dead" or pose == "GettingUp" or pose == "FallingDown" or pose == "Seated" or pose == "PlatformStanding") then
--		print("Wha " .. pose)
		stopAllAnimations()
		amplitude = 0.1
		frequency = 1
		setAngles = true
	end

	if (setAngles) then
		local desiredAngle = amplitude * math.sin(time * frequency)

		RightShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(desiredAngle + climbFudge)
		LeftShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(desiredAngle - climbFudge)
		RightHip:SetDesiredAngle(-desiredAngle)
		LeftHip:SetDesiredAngle(-desiredAngle)
	end

	-- Tool Animation handling
	local tool = getTool()
	if tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
	
		local animStringValueObject = getToolAnim(tool)

		if animStringValueObject then
			toolAnim = animStringValueObject.Value
			-- message recieved, delete StringValue
			animStringValueObject.Parent = nil
			toolAnimTime = time + .3
		end

		if time > toolAnimTime then
			toolAnimTime = 0
			toolAnim = "None"
		end

		animateTool()		
	else
		stopToolAnimations()
		toolAnim = "None"
		toolAnimInstance = nil
		toolAnimTime = 0
	end
end

-- connect events
Humanoid.Died:connect(onDied)
Humanoid.Running:connect(onRunning)
Humanoid.Jumping:connect(onJumping)
Humanoid.Climbing:connect(onClimbing)
Humanoid.GettingUp:connect(onGettingUp)
Humanoid.FreeFalling:connect(onFreeFall)
Humanoid.FallingDown:connect(onFallingDown)
Humanoid.Seated:connect(onSeated)
Humanoid.PlatformStanding:connect(onPlatformStanding)
Humanoid.Swimming:connect(onSwimming)

---- setup emote chat hook
game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Chatted:connect(function(msg)
	local emote = ""
	if msg == "/e dance" then
		emote = dances[math.random(1, #dances)]
	elseif (string.sub(msg, 1, 3) == "/e ") then
		emote = string.sub(msg, 4)
	elseif (string.sub(msg, 1, 7) == "/emote ") then
		emote = string.sub(msg, 8)
	end
	
	if (pose == "Standing" and emoteNames[emote] ~= nil) then
		playAnimation(emote, 0.1, Humanoid)
	end

end)

-- emote bindable hook
script:WaitForChild("PlayEmote").OnInvoke = function(emote)
	-- Only play emotes when idling
	if pose ~= "Standing" then
		return
	end
	if emoteNames[emote] ~= nil then
		-- Default emotes
		playAnimation(emote, EMOTE_TRANSITION_TIME, Humanoid)

		return true, currentAnimTrack
	end

	-- Return false to indicate that the emote could not be played
	return false
end
-- main program

-- initialize to idle
playAnimation("idle", 0.1, Humanoid)
pose = "Standing"

while Figure.Parent ~= nil do
	local _, time = wait(0.1)
	move(time)
end

		local animTrack = Instance.new("Animation")
		animTrack.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
		return animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack),
			animTrack:Destroy()
	end

		["robbed_idle"] = loadAnim(15530342903),
		["robbed"] = loadAnim(15530338211),
	}
	
	local function getRandomChild(parent)
		return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
	end
	
	local function cleanup()
		for index,event in pairs(connections) do
			event:Disconnect()
			connections[index] = nil
		end
		connections = nil
	end
	
	local ragdoll = RagdollModule.create(character, false)
	ragdoll:registerEvent()
	humanoid:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Ragdoll"):Connect(function()
		local state = humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll")
		ragdoll:set(state)
	end)
	
	for _,p in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
		if p:IsA("BasePart") then
			p.CollisionGroup = "Alive"
		end
	end
	
	table.insert(connections, listeners.beingAimAt.Changed:Connect(function()
		if listeners.beingAimAt.Value == true and not statements.surrender then
			statements.surrender = true
			character.cf_market.ableToBuy.Value = false
			if tick() - robbingTick >= config.forgetTime or robbingTick <= 0 then
				robbingTick = tick()
			end
			if tick() - robbingTick < config.robbingAwareTime + 0.5 then
				ChatService:Chat(character.Head, messages.robbing_start[math.random(#messages.robbing_start)])
			end
			anims.robbed_idle:Play(0.25)

			while humanoid.Health > 0 do
				if tick() - listeners.beingAimAt.update.Value >= config.robbingAwareTime then
					break
				end
				
				if tick() - robbingTick > config.robbingAwareTime + 1.5 and tick() - listeners.beingAimAt.update.Value <= 0.5 then

							bag.RotVelocity = humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.RightVector * -30
							bag.Name = "Duffel Bag"
							ChatService:Chat(character.Head, messages.robbed_finish[math.random(#messages.robbed_finish)])
							break
						end
						
						task.wait(1 / 10)
					end
				else
					anims.robbed_idle:Stop(0.25)
					ChatService:Chat(character.Head, messages.not_robbed[math.random(#messages.not_robbed)])
					listeners.beingAimAt.Value = false
					statements.surrender = false
					character.cf_market.ableToBuy.Value = true
				end
			end
		end
	end))

		cleanup()
		for _,p in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
			if p:IsA("BasePart") then
				p.CollisionGroup = "Death"
			end
		end

			humanoid.RootPart.CanCollide = false
			local deadLight = ClientStorage.Miscs.deadLight:Clone()
			deadLight.Parent = humanoid.RootPart
		end

		task.wait(config.respawnTime)
		clone.Parent = character.Parent
		character:Destroy()
	end))

	while true do
		if humanoid.RootPart and not humanoid.RootPart.Anchored then
			humanoid.RootPart:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
		end
		task.wait(1)
	end

local function deepCopy(original)
	local copy = {}
	for k, v in pairs(original) do
		if type(v) == "table" then
			v = deepCopy(v)
		end
		copy[k] = v
	end

	return copy
end

return function(result, target, character, humanoid, tool, settings, hitPart, dirV3, sessionProfile, finisher)
	if finisher then
		local damage = target.Humanoid.MaxHealth + 50
		target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)

		local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
		force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
		force.Velocity = (dirV3.unit + (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2)) * 30
		force.Parent = target.Humanoid.RootPart
		Debris:AddItem(force, 0.2)
		
		if target:FindFirstChild("Head") then
			local gib,_ = _G.getGibFromPosition(hitPart, result.Position)
			Network:FireAllClients("client", "gore", target, hitPart, damage, gib, dirV3, result.Position, target.Head.CFrame.LookVector, result.Normal, false)
		end
		
		--Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", Storage.Sounds.goal, humanoid.RootPart)
		--Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", Storage.Sounds.cheer, humanoid.RootPart)
	elseif target.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
		local knock = settings.knock and hitPart.Name == settings.knockPart
		if knock and sessionProfile.finish.Value == false then
			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
			force.Velocity = (dirV3.unit + (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2)) * settings.knockback
			force.Parent = hitPart
			Debris:AddItem(force, settings.knockFlingTime)
			
			Ragdoll.setGlobal(target, "muscles", true, hitPart, nil, settings.knockTime)
			task.wait(settings.knockTime)
			if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
				Ragdoll.setGlobal(target, "muscles", false, nil, 16e-5)
			end
		end
	end

local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
local StorageServer = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

local Ragdoll = require(StorageServer.Modules.Ragdoll)
local Network = require(Storage.Modules.Network)

local function deepCopy(original)
	local copy = {}
	for k, v in pairs(original) do
		if type(v) == "table" then
			v = deepCopy(v)
		end
		copy[k] = v
	end

	return copy
end

return function(result, target, character, humanoid, tool, settings, hitPart, dirV3, sessionProfile, finisher)
	if finisher then
		local damage = target.Humanoid.MaxHealth + 50
		target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)
		
		local gib,_ = _G.getGibFromPosition(hitPart, result.Position)
		Network:FireAllClients("client", "gore", target, hitPart, damage, gib, dirV3, result.Position, target.Head.CFrame.LookVector, result.Normal, false)
		if target:FindFirstChild("Head") and target.Head:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
			local sound = _G.createSound(Storage.Sounds.femur_breaker2, target.Head)
			sound.Name = "hurt"
			sound:Play()
			--Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", Storage.Sounds.femur_breaker2, target.Head)
		end
	elseif target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
		if hitPart:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
			hitPart:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
			force.Velocity = (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2) * math.random(60, 80)
			force.Parent = hitPart
			hitPart.RotVelocity = hitPart.Velocity
			Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
		end
	end
end  -  Editar

  03:10:35.974  ------------------------------------------  -  Editar
  03:10:35.974  PATH: ServerStorage.Storage.Modules.Finisher.Normal.The Crucible  -  Editar
  03:10:35.974  TYPE: ModuleScript  -  Editar
  03:10:35.974  ------------------------------------------  -  Editar
  03:10:35.974  local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
local StorageServer = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

local Ragdoll = require(StorageServer.Modules.Ragdoll)
local Network = require(Storage.Modules.Network)

local function deepCopy(original)
	local copy = {}
	for k, v in pairs(original) do
		if type(v) == "table" then
			v = deepCopy(v)
		end
		copy[k] = v
	end

	return copy
end

local function getDistance(p1, p2)
	return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
end

return function(result, target, character, humanoid, tool, settings, hitPart, dirV3, sessionProfile, finisher)
	if finisher then
		if hitPart:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
			hitPart:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
			force.Velocity = (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2) * math.random(25, 35)
			force.Parent = hitPart
			hitPart.RotVelocity = hitPart.Velocity
			Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
		end
		local damage = target.Humanoid.MaxHealth + 50
		target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)
		
		local sound = _G.createSound(Storage.Miscs.Weapons[tool.Name].Sounds.Lightning, target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position)
		_G.createFlashSFX(sound, 800, 250, 0.15)
		sound:Play()
		Network:FireAllClients("client", "crucibleStrike", hitPart.Position)
		task.spawn(function()
			local targets = _G.explode(hitPart.Position, {
				damage = 5,
				radius = 40,
				forceRagdoll = true
			}, {character = character, weapon = tool.Name .. " Explosion"}, {
				springConfig = {Damping = 1.1},
				dur = 0.8,
				min = 20,
				max = 100,
				strength = 130,

				blur = true,

				maxDist = 150,
			}, "CrucibleExplosionEffect", {target, character})
			
			for _,targetHit in pairs(targets) do
				local sparks = {}
				for _,v in pairs(targetHit:GetChildren()) do
					if v.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and v:IsA("BasePart") and v:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
						local spark = Storage.Particles.CrucibleSparks:Clone()
						spark.Parent = v
						sparks[#sparks + 1] = spark
						task.delay(2.5, function()
							spark.Enabled = false
						end)
					end
				end

				task.delay(4, function()
					for _,v in pairs(sparks) do
						v:Destroy()
					end
					table.clear(sparks)
				end)
				
				if targetHit.Humanoid.RootPart then
					Network:FireAllClients("client", "crucibleStrike", targetHit.Humanoid.RootPart.Position, hitPart.Position)
					Network:FireAllClients("client", "crucibleStrike", targetHit.Humanoid.RootPart.Position)
					_G.emitParticle("CrucibleHitEffect", nil, targetHit.Humanoid.RootPart.Position)
				end
			end
			
			table.clear(targets)
		end)
		
		local sparks = {}
		for _,v in pairs(target:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" and v:IsA("BasePart") and v:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
				local spark = Storage.Particles.CrucibleSparks:Clone()
				spark.Parent = v
				sparks[#sparks + 1] = spark
				task.delay(2.5, function()
					spark.Enabled = false
				end)
			end
		end
		
		task.delay(4, function()
			for _,v in pairs(sparks) do
				v:Destroy()
			end
			table.clear(sparks)
		end)
		
		if target:FindFirstChild("Head") then
			local gib,_ = _G.getGibFromPosition(hitPart, result.Position)
			Network:FireAllClients("client", "gore", target, hitPart, damage, gib, dirV3, result.Position, target.Head.CFrame.LookVector, result.Normal, false)
		end
		
		if target.Humanoid.RootPart then
			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
			force.Velocity = (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2) * 30
			force.Parent = target.Humanoid.RootPart
			Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
		end
	else
		if sessionProfile and sessionProfile.finish.Value == false or not character:GetAttribute("finish") then
			if hitPart.Name ~= "Right Arm" or target.Humanoid.Health <= 10 then
				hitPart:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
				hitPart.Velocity = (dirV3.unit + (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2)) * 60
				hitPart.RotVelocity = hitPart.Velocity / 2
			end
			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
			force.Velocity = (dirV3.unit + (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2)) * 40
			force.Parent = target:FindFirstChild("Head") and target.Head:GetAttribute("Enabled") and target.Head or target.Humanoid.RootPart
			Debris:AddItem(force, settings.knockFlingTime)

			if target.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
				Ragdoll.setGlobal(target, "muscles", true, hitPart, nil, settings.knockTime)
				task.wait(settings.knockTime)
				if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
					Ragdoll.setGlobal(target, "muscles", false, nil, 16e-5)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end  -  Editar

return function(result, target, character, humanoid, tool, settings, hitPart, dirV3, sessionProfile, finisher)
	if finisher then
		local damage = target.Humanoid.MaxHealth + 50
		target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)

		local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
		force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
		force.Velocity = (dirV3.unit + (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2)) * 30
		force.Parent = target.Humanoid.RootPart
		Debris:AddItem(force, 0.2)

			local gib,_ = _G.getGibFromPosition(hitPart, result.Position)
			Network:FireAllClients("client", "gore", target, hitPart, damage, gib, dirV3, result.Position, target.Head.CFrame.LookVector, result.Normal, false)
		end
		
		--Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", Storage.Sounds.goal, humanoid.RootPart)
		--Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", Storage.Sounds.cheer, humanoid.RootPart)
	elseif target.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
		local knock = settings.knock and hitPart.Name == settings.knockPart
		if knock and sessionProfile.finish.Value == false then
			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
			force.Velocity = (dirV3.unit + (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2)) * settings.knockback
			force.Parent = hitPart
			Debris:AddItem(force, settings.knockFlingTime)
			
			Ragdoll.setGlobal(target, "muscles", true, hitPart, nil, settings.knockTime)
			task.wait(settings.knockTime)
			if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
				Ragdoll.setGlobal(target, "muscles", false, nil, 16e-5)
			end
		end
	end

			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
			force.Velocity = (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2) * math.random(60, 80)
			force.Parent = hitPart
			hitPart.RotVelocity = hitPart.Velocity
			Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
		end
	end

local function deepCopy(original)
	local copy = {}
	for k, v in pairs(original) do
		if type(v) == "table" then
			v = deepCopy(v)
		end
		copy[k] = v
	end

	return copy
end

return function(result, target, character, humanoid, tool, settings, hitPart, dirV3, sessionProfile, finisher)
	if finisher then
		local damage = target.Humanoid.MaxHealth + 50
		target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)
		
		if target.Humanoid.RootPart then
			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
			force.Velocity = (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2) * 30
			force.Parent = target.Humanoid.RootPart
			Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
		end
		
		if target:FindFirstChild("Head") then
			local gib,_ = _G.getGibFromPosition(hitPart, result.Position)
			Network:FireAllClients("client", "gore", target, hitPart, damage, gib, dirV3, result.Position, target.Head.CFrame.LookVector, result.Normal, false)
		end
	elseif target.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
		local knock = settings.knock and hitPart.Name == settings.knockPart
		if knock and sessionProfile.finish.Value == false and math.random(4) == 1 then
			local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
			force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
			force.Velocity = (dirV3.unit + (humanoid.RootPart.CFrame.UpVector / 2)) * 5
			force.Parent = hitPart
			Debris:AddItem(force, settings.knockFlingTime)

			Ragdoll.setGlobal(target, "muscles", true, hitPart, nil, settings.knockTime)
			task.wait(settings.knockTime)
			if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
				Ragdoll.setGlobal(target, "muscles", false, nil, 16e-5)
			end
		end
	end

  03:10:35.977  return function(script)
	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")
	local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

	local ClientStorage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
	local Storage = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
	
	local CombatTag = require(ClientStorage.Modules.CombatTag)
	local Network = require(ClientStorage.Modules.Network)
	local WorldToGui = require(ClientStorage.Modules.WorldToGui)

	local character = script.Parent
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None

	local clone = character:Clone()
	character.Name = game:GetService("HttpService"):GenerateGUID(false)
	
	local connections = {}
	
	local function getRandomChild(parent)
		return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
	end

	local function isHumanoid(part)
		if part.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
			return part.Parent
		elseif part.Parent ~= workspace then
			return isHumanoid(part.Parent)
		end

		return false
	end

	local function getDistance(p1, p2)
		return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
	end

	local function emitParticle(name, multiplier, v, dur)
		local n = ClientStorage.Particles[name].Attachment:Clone()
		if typeof(v) == "Vector3" then
			n.Parent = workspace.Terrain
			n.WorldPosition = v
		else
			n.Parent = v
		end
		for _,p in pairs(n:GetChildren()) do
			p:Emit(p:GetAttribute("EmitCount") * (multiplier or 1))
		end
		Debris:AddItem(n, dur, 3)

		return n
	end

	local function createGuiSurface(texture, part, pos, scale, size, config)
		local surfaceFace, width, height, relativeX, relativeY = WorldToGui:WorldPositionToGuiPosition(part, pos)

		local surfaceGui = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
		surfaceGui.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(scale * width, scale * height)
		surfaceGui.LightInfluence = 1
		surfaceGui.Face = surfaceFace

		local surfaceFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
		surfaceFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		surfaceFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		surfaceFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
		surfaceFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		surfaceFrame.Parent = surfaceGui

		local decal = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		decal.Image = texture
		decal.BorderSizePixel = 0
		decal.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		decal.Size = UDim2.new(0, scale * size.x, 0, scale * size.y)
		decal.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		decal.Position = UDim2.new(relativeX, 0, relativeY, 0)
		for prop,data in pairs(config) do
			decal[prop] = data
		end
		decal.Parent = surfaceFrame

		surfaceGui.Parent = part
		Debris:AddItem(surfaceGui, 10)

		return surfaceGui
	end

	local burning = false
	table.insert(connections, humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(current)
		if (current / humanoid.MaxHealth) <= 50 and not burning and humanoid.Health > 0 and math.random(3) == 1 then
			for _,v in pairs(humanoid.RootPart.Attachment:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Sound") then
					v:Play()
				elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
					v.Enabled = true
				end
			end

			for k = 1, humanoid.MaxHealth do
				humanoid:TakeDamage(0.5)
				task.wait(0.5 + (math.random() * 0.5))
			end
		end
	end))
	
	local start = tick()
	local lastVelocity = humanoid.RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
	table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		if humanoid.RootPart and tick() - start >= 5 then
			if lastVelocity.magnitude - humanoid.RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.magnitude > 10 then
				humanoid:TakeDamage(humanoid.MaxHealth)
			end
			
			lastVelocity = humanoid.RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
		end
	end))
	
	table.insert(connections, humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
		for index,event in pairs(connections) do
			event:Disconnect()
			connections[index] = nil
		end
		connections = nil
		
		if humanoid.RootPart then
			for _,v in pairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
				if v:IsA("BasePart") then
					v.Transparency = 1
					v.CanCollide = false
					v.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
				elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or v:IsA("Light") then
					v.Enabled = false
				elseif v:IsA("Sound") then
					v:Stop()
				end
			end

			humanoid.RootPart.Size = Vector3.new()
			local params = RaycastParams.new()
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
			params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Include
			local crater = workspace:Raycast(humanoid.RootPart.Position, Vector3.new(0, -10, 0), params)
			if crater then
				local size = math.random(1200, 1500) / 100
				createGuiSurface(
					"rbxassetid://2828069358",
					crater.Instance,
					crater.Position,
					128,
					Vector2.new(size, size),
					{
						["ImageTransparency"] = math.random(150, 250) / 1000,
						["Rotation"] = 0,
						["ResampleMode"] = Enum.ResamplerMode.Pixelated,
					}
				)
			end

			Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", getRandomChild(ClientStorage.Sounds.explosions), humanoid.RootPart)

			local hitted = {}
			local explosion = Instance.new("Explosion")
			explosion.BlastPressure = 1.1
			explosion.BlastRadius = 29
			explosion.DestroyJointRadiusPercent = 0
			explosion.ExplosionType = Enum.ExplosionType.Craters
			explosion.Position = humanoid.RootPart.Position
			explosion.Parent = workspace.Terrain
			explosion.Hit:Connect(function(part)
				local target = isHumanoid(part)
				local player = target and game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)
				local pvp = player and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value or not player
				if target and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) and not hitted[target] and part.Parent == target and part:GetAttribute("Enabled") and not target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") and pvp then
					hitted[target] = true

					task.defer(function()
						local lastHealth = target.Humanoid.Health
						local dead = false

						local ragdoll = false
						local dist = getDistance(target.Humanoid.RootPart, explosion.Position)
						local baseDamage = 30
						local damage = explosion.BlastRadius / (dist / 2) * baseDamage

						if damage > 30 then
							ragdoll = true
						end

						local killer = CombatTag:getKiller(character)
						if killer and Players:FindFirstChild(killer) then
							if ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[killer].pvp.Value == false and target.Name ~= killer and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) then
								return
							end
							
							CombatTag:addTag(target, Players[killer].Character, {damage = damage, weapon = "Explosive Barrel"})
						end
						target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(damage)

						if target.Humanoid.Health <= 0 and lastHealth > 0 then
							dead = true
						end

						local parts = {}
						if dead then
							for _,part in pairs(target:GetChildren()) do
								if part:IsA("BasePart") then
									local params = RaycastParams.new()
									params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {target}
									params.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"
									params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist

									local origin = part.Position + (part.CFrame.LookVector * -1)
									local dir = part.CFrame.LookVector * 2
									local cast = workspace:Raycast(origin, dir, params)
									if cast then
										local gib,_ = _G.getGibFromPosition(cast.Instance, cast.Position)
										Network:FireAllClients("client", "gore", target, cast.Instance, damage, gib, (target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position - humanoid.RootPart.Position).unit * 2, cast.Position, target.Head.CFrame.LookVector, cast.Normal, false)
									end

									if math.random(5) == 1 then
										parts[#parts + 1] = part
									end
								end
							end
						end

						if ragdoll then
							target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 3.5)
							target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", true)

							local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
							force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
							force.Velocity = (target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position - humanoid.RootPart.Position).unit * math.max(damage / 4, 15) + Vector3.new(0, math.random(8, 12), 0)
							force.Parent = part
							Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
							if part:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
								target.Humanoid.RootPart.Velocity = part.Velocity / 2
							end
						end
						emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position)

						if dead then
							task.defer(function()
								for _,part in pairs(parts) do
									if part:GetAttribute("Limbs") then
										part:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
										_G.createSound(getRandomChild(ClientStorage.Sounds.fleshHits), part):Play()
										emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, part.Position)
									end
								end
							end)
						end
					end)
				elseif target and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Objects) then
					task.wait(1 / 15)
					local killer = CombatTag:getKiller(character)
					if killer and Players:FindFirstChild(killer) then
						CombatTag:addTag(target, Players[killer].Character, {damage = 1000, weapon = "Explosive Barrel"})
					end	
					target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(1000)
				elseif not target and part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass) then
					ClientStorage.Events.glass:Fire(part, part.Position, (part.Position - humanoid.RootPart.Position).unit * 30 + Vector3.new(0, math.random(8, 12), 0))
				end
			end)

			local connection
			connection = explosion.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
				if parent == nil then
					connection:Disconnect()
					table.clear(hitted)
				end
			end)

			for _,player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				local pChar = player.Character
				local pHum = pChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
				if pHum.Health > 0 and pHum.RootPart and getDistance(pHum.RootPart, humanoid.RootPart) <= 150 then
					local dist = getDistance(pHum.RootPart, humanoid.RootPart)
					ClientStorage.Events.shake:FireClient(player, {Damping = 1.2}, math.clamp(80 - dist, 5, 60), 0.8, true)
				end
			end
		end

		task.wait(8)
		clone.Parent = character.Parent
		character:Destroy()
	end))
	
	humanoid.PlatformStand = true
end  -  Editar

module.__index = module

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Assets = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

local limbsToMotor = {
	["Head"] = "Neck",
	["Right Arm"] = "Right Shoulder",
	["Right Leg"] = "Right Hip",
	["Left Arm"] = "Left Shoulder",
	["Left Leg"] = "Left Hip",
}

local motorToLimbs = {
	["Neck"] = "Neck",
	["Right Shoulder"] = "Right Arm",
	["Right Hip"] = "Right Leg",
	["Left Shoulder"] = "Left Arm",
	["Left Hip"] = "Left Leg",
}

local config = {
	debug = false;
}

local euphoriaAnims = {
	["Normal"] = {
		15509055044,
	};
	["Arm"] = {
		["Left Arm"] = {
			15509055930,
			15509057599
		},
		["Right Arm"] = {
			15509058388,
			15509059242
		},
	};
	["Head"] = {
		15509060230,

	};
	["Leg"] = {
		["Right Leg"] = {
