		},
		["Left Leg"] = {
			15509066325,
		}
	};
	
	["Burning"] = {15509067368},
}

local function getRandomChild(parent)
	return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
end

function module.create(character, euphoria)
	local player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
	humanoid.RequiresNeck = false
	humanoid:SetAttribute("Euphoria", false)
	humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", false)
	humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 0)
	task.delay(1 / 30, function()
		local clientScript = player and script.LocalScript:Clone()
		if player then
			clientScript.Parent = player.PlayerGui
			clientScript.Enabled = true
		end
	end)
	local ragdollStuff = {}
	local collisions = {}
	local muscles = euphoria and Assets.Ragdoll.Muscles.Bones:Clone()
	local musclesSprings = {}
	local euphoriaState = false
	if muscles then
		muscles.Name = character.Name
		muscles.Parent = workspace.Ignored.Muscles
		muscles.Humanoid.PlatformStand = true
		local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
		weld.Part0 = character.Torso
		weld.Part1 = muscles.Torso
		weld.Parent = muscles
		if muscles.Torso:CanSetNetworkOwnership() then
			muscles.Torso:SetNetworkOwner(Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character))
		end
		if config.debug then
			for _,part in pairs(muscles:GetDescendants()) do
				if part:IsA("BasePart") and part ~= muscles.Humanoid.RootPart then
					part.Transparency = 0.75
				elseif part:IsA("Attachment") then
					part.Visible = true
				elseif part:IsA("Beam") then
					part.Enabled = true
				end
			end
		end
		
		for _,instance in pairs(muscles:GetDescendants()) do
			if instance:IsA("Attachment") then
				local charBone = character:FindFirstChild(instance.Name, true)
				local charPart = charBone and character:FindFirstChild(instance.Parent.Name)
				if charPart then
					if not musclesSprings[charPart] then
						musclesSprings[charPart] = {}
					end
					local spring = Instance.new("SpringConstraint")
					spring.Attachment0 = instance
					spring.Attachment1 = charBone
					spring.LimitsEnabled = true
					spring.MaxLength = instance.Length.Value
					spring.Enabled = false
					spring.Name = instance.Parent.Name .. "-" .. tick()
					spring.Parent = instance.Parent
					musclesSprings[charPart][#musclesSprings[charPart] + 1] = spring
					
					local bone2 = instance.Parent:FindFirstChild(instance.Name .. "1")
					if bone2 then
						local a0 = bone2:Clone()
						a0.Parent = charBone.Parent
						local spring = Instance.new("SpringConstraint")
						spring.Attachment0 = a0
						spring.Attachment1 = instance.Parent[instance.Name .. "1"]
						spring.LimitsEnabled = true
						spring.MaxLength = bone2.Length.Value
						spring.Enabled = false
						spring.Name = instance.Parent.Name .. "-" .. tick()
						spring.Parent = instance.Parent
						musclesSprings[charPart][#musclesSprings[charPart] + 1] = spring
					end
				end
			elseif instance:IsA("BasePart") then
				instance.CollisionGroup = "NoCollisions"
			end
		end
	end

	for _,part in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
		local validPart = Assets.Ragdoll:FindFirstChild(part.Name)
		if validPart then
			part:SetAttribute("Health", 100)
			part:SetAttribute("MaxHealth", 100)
			part:SetAttribute("Enabled", true)
			part:SetAttribute("Limbs", true)
			part.CanCollide = false
			local motor = character.Torso:FindFirstChild(limbsToMotor[part.Name])
			local a0, a1 = validPart["0"]:Clone(), validPart["1"]:Clone()
			local socket = validPart.Socket:Clone()
			socket.Attachment0 = a0
			socket.Attachment1 = a1
			socket.Parent = part
			a0.Parent = character.Torso
			a1.Parent = part
			
			ragdollStuff[part] = {
				a0 = a0,
				a1 = a1,
				socket = socket
			}
			
			local collisionPart = validPart.collision:Clone()
			collisionPart.CanCollide = false
			collisionPart.Weld.Part0 = part
			collisionPart.Name = part.Name .. "_CD"
			collisionPart.Parent = part
			collisions[part] = collisionPart
			
			local REGEN_RATE = 1/150
			local REGEN_STEP = 1
			
			local regen = false
			local limbBreak = false
			part:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
				local unit = part:GetAttribute("Health")
				if unit <= 0 and not limbBreak and part:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
					limbBreak = true
					local sound = _G.createSound(Assets.Sounds.limb_breaks, part)
					if sound then
						sound:Play()
					end
				elseif unit >= part:GetAttribute("MaxHealth") then
					limbBreak = false
				end
				
				if not regen and part:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
					regen = true
					while unit < part:GetAttribute("MaxHealth") and part:GetAttribute("Enabled") do
						local dt = wait(REGEN_STEP)
						local dh = dt*REGEN_RATE*part:GetAttribute("MaxHealth")
						if not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") then
							part:SetAttribute("Health", math.min(part:GetAttribute("Health") + dh, part:GetAttribute("MaxHealth")))
						end
					end
					
					regen = false
				end
			end)
			
			part:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
				local state = part:GetAttribute("Enabled")
				if not state and part.Name == "Head" then
					humanoid.Health = 0
				end
				
				collisionPart.CanCollide = not state
				if humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and state then
					socket.Enabled = state
					if musclesSprings[part] then
						for _,spring in pairs(musclesSprings[part]) do
							spring.Enabled = state
						end
					end
				else
					socket.Enabled = false
					if musclesSprings[part] then
						for _,spring in pairs(musclesSprings[part]) do
							spring.Enabled = false
						end
					end
					
					if not state then
						if character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
							for _,sound in pairs(character.Head:GetChildren()) do
								if (sound.Name == "hurt") then
									sound:Destroy()
								end
							end
							
							if humanoid.Health > 0 then
								local sound = _G.createSound(getRandomChild(Assets.Sounds.hurt), character.Head, nil, nil, 0.25)
								if sound then
									sound.Name = "hurt"
									sound:Play()
								end
							end
						end
						
						part.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
					end
				end
				
				if motor then
					motor:SetAttribute("Enabled", state)
					motor.Enabled = state
				end
			end)
			
			if motor then
				motor:SetAttribute("Enabled", true)
			end
		end
	end
	
	local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local connections = {}
	local forces = {}
	
	table.insert(connections, humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
		event:Fire("switch", true, euphoriaState)
	end))
	
	humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", false)
	local function charFunc(type, ...)
		if type == "clean" then
			for index,con in pairs(connections) do
				con:Disconnect()
				connections[index] = nil
			end
			for index,data in pairs(ragdollStuff) do
				for _,v in pairs(data) do
					if typeof(v) == "Instance" then
						v:Destroy()
					end
				end
				table.clear(data)
				ragdollStuff[index] = nil
			end
			
			if muscles then
				muscles:Destroy()
			end
			
			event:Destroy()
		elseif type == "switch" then
			local boolean, euphoria = ...
			
			if character:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", boolean)
				
				for _,motor in pairs(character.Torso:GetChildren()) do
					if motor:IsA("Motor6D") and motor:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
						motor.Enabled = not boolean
					end
				end
				
				if boolean and euphoria then
					for part,data in pairs(ragdollStuff) do
						if part.Name ~= "Head" then
							data.socket.Enabled = false
						end
					end
				else
					for part,data in pairs(ragdollStuff) do
						if part:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
							data.socket.Enabled = boolean
						end
					end
				end
				
				for part,collisionPart in pairs(collisions) do
					if part:GetAttribute("Enabled") then
						collisionPart.CanCollide = boolean
					else
						collisionPart.CanCollide = true
					end
				end
				
				-- // ragdoll state
				if not player then
					if boolean then
						if #forces < 1 then
							for _,part in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
								if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
									local force = Instance.new("BodyForce")
									force.Force = Vector3.new(0, part:GetMass() * 50, 0)
									force.Parent = part
									forces[#forces + 1] = force
								end
							end
						end
						
						humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, false)
						humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
						humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall, false)
						humanoid.PlatformStand = true
					else
						for index,force in pairs(forces) do
							force:Destroy()
							forces[index] = nil
						end
						forces = {}
						humanoid.PlatformStand = false
						humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
						local connection
						connection = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_,current)
							if current == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp or humanoid.Health < 1 then
								humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
								humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
								humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall, true)
								connection:Disconnect()
							end
						end)
					end
				end
				
				if not boolean then
					humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 0)
				end
			end
		elseif type == "muscles" then
			local state, part, disableTime, playTime = ...
			for part,data in pairs(musclesSprings) do
				if state or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
					if musclesSprings[part] then
						for _,spring in pairs(musclesSprings[part]) do
							spring.Enabled = part:GetAttribute("Enabled")
						end
					end
				else
					task.delay(disableTime or 16e-6, function()
						if musclesSprings[part] then
							for _,spring in pairs(musclesSprings[part]) do
								spring.Enabled = false
							end
						end
					end)
				end
			end
			
			euphoriaState = state
			humanoid:SetAttribute("Euphoria", state)
			event:Fire("switch", state or humanoid.Health <= 0, true)
			if state then
				for _,anim in pairs(muscles.Humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					anim:Stop()
				end
				local animId
				local animName = typeof(part) == "string" and part or typeof(part) == "Instance" and part.Name
				for index,t in pairs(euphoriaAnims) do
					if string.find(animName, index) then
						animId = t[animName] and t[animName][math.random(#t[animName])] or t[math.random(#t)]	
						break
					else
						animId = euphoriaAnims.Normal[math.random(#euphoriaAnims.Normal)]
					end
				end
				local animTrack = Instance.new("Animation")
				animTrack.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. animId
				local anim = muscles.Humanoid.Animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack)
				anim:Play()
				anim:AdjustWeight(math.random(8, 10) / 10)
				animTrack:Destroy()
				task.spawn(function()
					local startTick = tick()
					while euphoriaState and character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and tick() - startTick < playTime - 0.5 do
						task.wait(1 / 15)
					end
					anim:AdjustWeight(0, math.min(playTime, 2) - 0.5)
				end)
			elseif muscles:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
				for _,anim in pairs(muscles.Humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					anim:Stop(0.5)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	
	table.insert(connections, event.Event:Connect(function(...)
		charFunc(...)
	end))
	
	return setmetatable({
		character = character,
		humanoid = humanoid,
		event = event,
		ragdoll = false,
		euphoria = false,
		dur = 0,
		connections = connections
	}, module)
end

function module.setGlobal(...)
	script._signal:Fire(...)
end

function module:registerEvent()
	table.insert(self.connections, script._signal.Event:Connect(function(char, func, ...)
		if char == self.character then
			if func == "set" then
				self:set(...)
			elseif func == "setLimb" then
				self:setLimb(...)
			elseif func == "muscles" then
				self:muscles(func, ...)
			end
		end
	end))
end

function module:getTime()
	return os.clock() - self.dur
end

function module:set(state, timer)
	if state then
		self.dur = os.clock()
		--timer = self.humanoid:GetAttribute("RagdollDur") > 0 and self.humanoid:GetAttribute("RagdollDur") or nil
	else
		self.ragdoll = false
		return
	end
	
	if not self.ragdoll then
		self.ragdoll = true
		self.event:Fire("switch", true, self.euphoria)
		
		task.spawn(function()
			while self.character and self.character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and self.humanoid.Health > 0 and self:getTime() < (self.humanoid:GetAttribute("RagdollDur") > 0 and self.humanoid:GetAttribute("RagdollDur") or math.huge) and self.ragdoll do
				task.wait(1 / 30)
			end
			if self.humanoid and self.humanoid.Health > 0 and not self.euphoria then
				self.ragdoll = false
				if self.character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
					self.event:Fire("switch", false, self.euphoria)
				else
					self:destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

function module:disableLimb(limb, state)
	local part = self.character:FindFirstChild(limb)
	if part then
		part:SetAttribute("Disabled", state)
	end
end

function module:muscles(func, state, ...)
	self.euphoria = state
	self.event:Fire(func, state, ...)
end

function module:destroy()
	--self.character:Destroy()
	self.event:Fire("clean")
	for index,_ in pairs(self) do
		self[index] = nil
	end
end

function module:Destroy()
	return self:destroy()
end

  03:10:35.978  local module = {}
module.__index = module

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Assets = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

local limbsToMotor = {
	["Head"] = "Neck",
	["Right Arm"] = "Right Shoulder",
	["Right Leg"] = "Right Hip",
	["Left Arm"] = "Left Shoulder",
	["Left Leg"] = "Left Hip",
}

local motorToLimbs = {
	["Neck"] = "Neck",
	["Right Shoulder"] = "Right Arm",
	["Right Hip"] = "Right Leg",
	["Left Shoulder"] = "Left Arm",
	["Left Hip"] = "Left Leg",
}

local config = {
	debug = false;
}

local euphoriaAnims = {
	["Normal"] = {
		15517367813,
	};
	["Arm"] = {
		["Left Arm"] = {
			15517370023,
			15517371415
		},

		}

function module.create(character, euphoria)
	local player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	humanoid.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
	task.delay(1 / 30, function()
		local clientScript = player and script.LocalScript:Clone()
		if player then
			clientScript.Parent = player.PlayerGui
			clientScript.Enabled = true
		end
	end)
	local ragdollStuff = {}
	local collisions = {}
	local muscles = euphoria and Assets.Ragdoll.Muscles.Bones:Clone()
	local musclesSprings = {}
	local euphoriaState = false
	if (muscles) then
		local connection
		connection = character.Destroying:Connect(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
			muscles:Destroy()
		end)
		muscles.Name = character.Name .. "__muscles"
		muscles.Parent = workspace.Ignored.Muscles
		muscles.Humanoid.PlatformStand = true
		local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
		weld.Part0 = character.Torso
		weld.Part1 = muscles.Torso
		weld.Parent = character
		if (config.debug) then
			for _,part in pairs(muscles:GetDescendants()) do
				if (part:IsA("BasePart") and part ~= muscles.Humanoid.RootPart) then
					part.Transparency = 0.75
				elseif (part:IsA("Attachment")) then
					part.Visible = true
				elseif (part:IsA("Beam")) then
					part.Enabled = true
				end
			end
		end
		
		for _,bone in pairs(muscles:GetDescendants()) do
			if (bone:IsA("Attachment")) then
				local charBone = character:FindFirstChild(bone.Name, true)
				if (charBone) then
					local spring = Instance.new("SpringConstraint")
					spring.Attachment0 = bone
					spring.Attachment1 = charBone
					spring.LimitsEnabled = true
					spring.MaxLength = bone.Length.Value
					spring.Enabled = false
					spring.Name = bone.Parent.Name
					spring.Parent = bone.Parent
					musclesSprings[#musclesSprings + 1] = spring
					
					local bone2 = bone.Parent:FindFirstChild(bone.Name .. "1")
					if (bone2) then
						local a0 = bone2:Clone()
						a0.Parent = charBone.Parent
						local spring = Instance.new("SpringConstraint")
						spring.Attachment0 = a0
						spring.Attachment1 = bone.Parent[bone.Name .. "1"]
						spring.LimitsEnabled = true
						spring.MaxLength = bone2.Length.Value
						spring.Enabled = false
						spring.Name = bone.Parent.Name
						spring.Parent = bone.Parent
						musclesSprings[#musclesSprings + 1] = spring
					end
				end
			end
		end
		
		--humanoid.RootPart:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
	end
	for _,part in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
		local validPart = Assets.Ragdoll:FindFirstChild(part.Name)
		if (validPart) then
			part.CanCollide = false
			local a0, a1 = validPart["0"]:Clone(), validPart["1"]:Clone()
			local socket = validPart.Socket:Clone()
			socket.Attachment0 = a0
			socket.Attachment1 = a1
			socket.Parent = part
			a0.Parent = character.Torso
			a1.Parent = part
			a0.Name = "_RD"
			a1.Name = "_RD"
			ragdollStuff[#ragdollStuff + 1] = a0
			ragdollStuff[#ragdollStuff + 1] = a1
			ragdollStuff[#ragdollStuff + 1] = socket
			
			local collisionPart = validPart.collision:Clone()
			collisionPart.Weld.Part0 = part
			collisionPart.Name = part.Name
			collisionPart.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
			collisionPart.CanCollide = false
			collisionPart.Parent = part
			collisions[#collisions + 1] = collisionPart
		end
	end
	
	local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local connections = {}
	local forces = {}
	
	table.insert(connections, humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
		event:Fire("switch", true, euphoriaState)
	end))
	
	humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", false)
	local function charFunc(type, ...)
		if (type == "clean") then
			for index,con in pairs(connections) do
				con:Disconnect()
				connections[index] = nil
			end
			for index,stuff in pairs(ragdollStuff) do
				stuff:Destroy()
				ragdollStuff[index] = nil
			end
			event:Destroy()
		elseif (type == "switch") then
			local boolean, euphoria, part = ...
			if (character:FindFirstChild("Torso")) then
				humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", boolean)
				local noMess = {}
				for _,motor in pairs(character.Torso:GetChildren()) do
					if (motor:IsA("Motor6D") and not motor:FindFirstChild("no")) then
						motor.Enabled = not boolean
					elseif (motor:FindFirstChild("no")) then
						noMess[motor.Name] = true
					end
				end
				
				if (boolean and euphoria) then
					for _,socket in pairs(ragdollStuff) do
						if (socket:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") and socket.Parent.Name ~= "Head" --[[and socket.Parent ~= part]]) then
							socket.Enabled = false
						end
					end
				else
					for _,socket in pairs(ragdollStuff) do
						if (socket:IsA("BallSocketConstraint")) then
							socket.Enabled = true
						end
					end
				end
				
				for _,part in pairs(collisions) do
					if (not noMess[part.Name]) then
						part.CanCollide = boolean
					end
				end
				
				-- // ragdoll state
				if (not player) then
					if (boolean) then
						if (#forces < 1) then
							for _,part in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
								if (part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart") then
									local force = Instance.new("BodyForce")
									force.Force = Vector3.new(0, part:GetMass() * 50, 0)
									force.Parent = part
									forces[#forces + 1] = force
								end
							end
						end
						
						humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, false)
						humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false)
						humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall, false)
						humanoid.PlatformStand = true
					else
						for index,force in pairs(forces) do
							force:Destroy()
							forces[index] = nil
						end
						forces = {}
						humanoid.PlatformStand = false
						humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
						local connection
						connection = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_,current)
							if (current == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp or humanoid.Health < 1) then
								humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
								humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true)
								humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall, true)
								connection:Disconnect()
							end
						end)
					end
				end
				
				--humanoid.RootPart:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
				if not boolean then
					humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 0)
				end
			end
		elseif (type == "muscles") then
			local state, part, disableTime, playTime = ...
			for _,spring in pairs(musclesSprings) do
				--[[if ((state and spring.Name ~= part.Name) or humanoid.Health <= 0) then
					spring.Enabled = true
				else
					task.delay(disableTime or 16e-6, function()
						spring.Enabled = false
					end)
				end]]
				
				if (state or humanoid.Health <= 0) then
					spring.Enabled = true
				else
					task.delay(disableTime or 16e-6, function()
						spring.Enabled = false
					end)
				end
			end
			euphoriaState = state
			event:Fire("switch", (state or humanoid.Health <= 0), true, part)
			if (state) then
				for _,anim in pairs(muscles.Humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					anim:Stop()
				end
				local animId
				for index,t in pairs(euphoriaAnims) do
					if (string.find(part.Name, index)) then
						animId = t[part.Name] and t[part.Name][math.random(#t[part.Name])] or t[math.random(#t)]	
						break
					else
						animId = euphoriaAnims.Normal[math.random(#euphoriaAnims.Normal)]
					end
				end
				local animTrack = Instance.new("Animation")
				animTrack.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. animId
				local anim = muscles.Humanoid.Animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack)
				anim:Play()
				anim:AdjustWeight(math.random(8, 10) / 10)
				animTrack:Destroy()
				task.spawn(function()
					local startTick = tick()
					while euphoriaState and character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and tick() - startTick < playTime - 0.5 do
						task.wait(1 / 30)
					end
					anim:AdjustWeight(0, playTime - 0.5)
				end)
			else
				for _,anim in pairs(muscles.Humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
					anim:Stop(0.5)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	
	table.insert(connections, event.Event:Connect(function(...)
		charFunc(...)
	end))
	
	return setmetatable({
		character = character,
		humanoid = humanoid,
		event = event,
		ragdoll = false,
		euphoria = false,
		dur = 0,
		connections = connections
	}, module)
end

function module.setGlobal(...)
	script._signal:Fire(...)
end

function module:registerEvent()
	table.insert(self.connections, script._signal.Event:Connect(function(char, func, ...)
		if (char == self.character) then
			if (func == "set") then
				self:set(...)
			elseif (func == "setLimb") then
				self:setLimb(...)
			elseif (func == "muscles") then
				self:muscles(func, ...)
			end
		end
	end))
end

function module:getTime()
	return tick() - self.dur
end

function module:set(state, timer)
	if (state) then
		self.dur = os.clock()
		timer = self.humanoid:GetAttribute("RagdollDur") > 0 and self.humanoid:GetAttribute("RagdollDur") or nil
	else
		self.ragdoll = false
		return
	end

	if (not self.ragdoll) then
		self.ragdoll = true
		self.event:Fire("switch", true, self.euphoria)

		task.spawn(function()
			while self.character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and self.humanoid.Health > 0 and self:getTime() < (timer or math.huge) and self.ragdoll do
				task.wait(1 / 30)
			end

			if (self.humanoid.Health > 0 and not self.euphoria) then
				self.ragdoll = false
				if (self.character:IsDescendantOf(workspace)) then
					self.event:Fire("switch", false, self.euphoria)
				else
					self:destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end
end

function module:setLimb(limb, state)
	local motor = self.character.Torso[limbsToMotor[limb]]
	motor.Enabled = state
	if (not state and not motor:FindFirstChild("no")) then
		local no = Instance.new("StringValue")
		no.Name = "no"
		no.Parent = motor
	elseif (state) then
		local findMotor = motor:FindFirstChild("no")
		if (findMotor) then findMotor:Destroy() end
	end
end

function module:muscles(func, state, ...)
	self.euphoria = state
	self.event:Fire(func, state, ...)
end

function module:destroy()
	--self.character:Destroy()
	self.event:Fire("clean")
	for index,_ in pairs(self) do
		self[index] = nil
	end
end

function module:Destroy()
	return self:destroy()
end

	local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")
	local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

	local ClientStorage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
	local Storage = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
	
	local RagdollModule = require(Storage.Modules.Ragdoll)
	local Network = require(ClientStorage.Modules.Network)
	local CombatTag = require(ClientStorage.Modules.CombatTag)
	local CharacterEffects = require(Storage.Modules.CharacterEffects)

	local character = script.Parent
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")
	--humanoid.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None

	local clone = character:Clone()
	character.Name = "ERROR-" .. math.random(1000, 9999)
	
	local loadAvatar = false
	
	local function getRandomChild(parent)
		return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
	end
	
	local regenScript = ClientStorage.Miscs.Health:Clone()
	regenScript.Parent = character
	regenScript.Enabled = true
	
	local animateScript = ClientStorage.Miscs.Animate:Clone()
	animateScript.Parent = character
	animateScript.Enabled = true
	
	local connections = {}
	
	humanoid:SetAttribute("Downed", false)
	humanoid:SetAttribute("Carried", false)
	local downedSFX = ClientStorage.Sounds.downed:Clone()
	downedSFX.Parent = character.Head
	local ragdoll
	
	local function loadAnim(id)
		local animTrack = Instance.new("Animation")
		animTrack.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
		return animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack),
			animTrack:Destroy()
	end
	
	local downed_anim = loadAnim(15509084290)
	local lastHealth = humanoid.Health
	table.insert(connections, humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(hp)
		local damage = lastHealth - hp
		if hp > 5 and (hp / humanoid.MaxHealth) * 100 < 15 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and damage < 80 then
			humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 2.5)
			humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", true)
			humanoid:SetAttribute("Downed", true)
		end
		
		lastHealth = humanoid.Health
	end))
	
	table.insert(connections, humanoid:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Downed"):Connect(function()
		local downed = humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed")
		if downed then
			humanoid.Health = humanoid.MaxHealth
			humanoid.WalkSpeed = 2
			downed_anim:Play(0.5)
			humanoid.HipHeight = -2
		else
			humanoid.Health = 30
			humanoid.WalkSpeed = game:GetService("StarterPlayer").CharacterWalkSpeed
			downed_anim:Stop()
			humanoid.HipHeight = 0
			downedSFX:Stop()
		end
		
		local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
		if torso then
			torso.CollisionGroup = downed and "NoCollisions" or (humanoid.Health > 0 and "Alive" or "Death")
		end

		if humanoid.RootPart then
			humanoid.RootPart.CanCollide = downed
		end
	end))
	
	for _,p in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
		if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
			p.CollisionGroup = "Alive"
		elseif p.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
			p.CollisionGroup = "CollisionNoRaycast"
		end
	end

	table.insert(connections, humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
		downedSFX:Stop()
		for index,event in pairs(connections) do
			connections[index]:Disconnect()
			connections[index] = nil
		end
		
		for _,p in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
			if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
				p.CollisionGroup = "Death"
			elseif p.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
				p.CollisionGroup = "CollisionNoRaycast"
			end
		end
		
		if character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
			for _,sound in pairs(character.Head:GetChildren()) do
				if sound.Name == "hurt" then
					sound:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end
		
		if humanoid.RootPart then
			Network:FireAllClients("client", "createSound", getRandomChild(ClientStorage.Sounds.died), humanoid.RootPart)
			humanoid.RootPart.CanCollide = false
			local deadLight = ClientStorage.Miscs.deadLight:Clone()
			deadLight.Parent = humanoid.RootPart
		end
		
		local killer, damage, weapon = CombatTag:getKiller(character)
		if killer and Players:FindFirstChild(killer) then
			Network:FireClient(Players[killer], "client", "killFX", character.Name, weapon)
			ClientStorage.Events.kill:Fire(Players[killer], "dummy")
			
		end

		task.wait(8)
		clone.Parent = character.Parent
		
		task.wait(10)
		if ragdoll then
			ragdoll:Destroy()
		end
		character:Destroy()
	end))
	
	if loadAvatar then
		pcall(function()
			local response = HttpService:GetAsync("https://groups.roproxy.com/v1/groups/17197064/users?sortOrder=Asc&limit=50")
			response = HttpService:JSONDecode(response)

			local userIds = {}
			for i, memberData in pairs(response.data) do
				table.insert(userIds, memberData.user.userId)
			end

			local defaultId
			local chosenId = userIds[math.random(#userIds)]
			local Name = Players:GetNameFromUserIdAsync(defaultId or chosenId)
			local Description = Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(defaultId or chosenId)
			humanoid:ApplyDescription(Description)
			--humanoid.DisplayName = Name
		end)
	end

	for _,v in pairs(script.Parent:GetDescendants()) do
		if (v:IsA("BasePart") and (v.Parent:IsA("Accessory") or v.Parent.Parent:IsA("Accessory"))) then
			v.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
		elseif(v:IsA("CharacterMesh")) then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	
	ragdoll = RagdollModule.create(character, true)
	ragdoll:registerEvent()
	table.insert(connections, humanoid:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Ragdoll"):Connect(function()
		local state = humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll")
		ragdoll:set(state)
		if not state and humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") then
			downedSFX:Play()
		end

		local downed = humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed")
		local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
		if torso then
			if downed and humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") then
				torso.CollisionGroup = humanoid.Health > 0 and "Alive" or "Death"
			elseif downed and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") then
				torso.CollisionGroup = "NoCollisionsRaycast"
			end
		end
	end))
	CharacterEffects:createEffects(humanoid)

	if humanoid.RootPart and not humanoid.RootPart.Anchored then
		humanoid.RootPart:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
	end

	local function loadAnim(id)

		return animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack),
			animTrack:Destroy()
	end

	local anims = {}
	for animName,id in pairs(settings.anims) do
		anims[animName] = loadAnim(id)
	end

	local active = false
	local used = tool._data.Eaten

	local function getDistance(p1, p2)
		return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
	end

	local function getRandomChild(parent)
		return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
	end

	local equipTick = 0

		return tick() - equipTick >= settings.equipTime
	end

	tool.Equipped:Connect(function()
		equipTick = tick()

		anims.equip.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
		anims.equip:Play(0.2)
		anims.idle:Play(0.2)
		
		table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
			if not process then
				if inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and not active and humanoid.Health > 0 and isEquipped() and not used.Value then
					active = true
					_G.foodUse = true
					while anims.use.Length <= 0 and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and active do
						task.wait()
					end
					if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and anims.use.Length > 0 and active then
						anims.use:Play(0.1)
						tool.Handle.Value.eat:Play()
						UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.progress.Visible = true
						local startTime = tick()
						while active and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and humanoid.Health > 0 do
							UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.progress.display.Text = "EATING " .. string.upper(tool.Name) .. "..."
							UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.progress.bar.Size = UDim2.new((anims.use.TimePosition / anims.use.Length) * 1, 0, 1, 0)
							if anims.use.TimePosition >= anims.use.Length then
								tool.Event:FireServer()
								tool.Parent = player.Backpack
								
								local saturation = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
								saturation.Brightness = 0.1
								saturation.Saturation = 0
								saturation.Parent = camera
								TweenService:Create(saturation, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Saturation = 0, Brightness = 0}):Play()
								Debris:AddItem(saturation, 1)
								
								break
							end
							task.wait(1 / 15)
						end
						
						tool.Handle.Value.eat:Stop()
						anims.use:Stop()
						UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.statsInfo.progress.Visible = false
					end
					
					_G.foodUse = false
				end
			end
		end))

		table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inputObject)
			if inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
				active = false
			end
		end))
	end)

	tool.Unequipped:Connect(function()
		active = false

		for i,v in pairs(connections) do
			v:Disconnect()
			connections[i] = nil
		end

		for _,v in pairs(anims) do
			v:AdjustWeight(0)
			v:Stop()
		end
	end)

	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")

	local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
	local StorageServer = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

	local tool = script.Parent
	local player = tool.Parent.Parent

	local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Foods:WaitForChild(tool.Name))

	local character = player:IsA("Player") and (player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()) or tool.Parent
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

	tool.Motor.Part0 = character:WaitForChild("Right Arm") or character:WaitForChild("Left Arm")
	for _,v in pairs(tool._data:GetChildren()) do
		if settings[v.Name] then
			v.Value = settings[v.Name]
		end
	end
	
	tool.Event.OnServerEvent:Connect(function()
		if not tool._data.Eaten.Value then
			humanoid.Health += settings.healthRegen
		end
		tool._data.Eaten.Value = true
		task.delay(0.5,function()
			tool:Destroy()
		end)
	end)

	if player:IsA("Player") then
		local connection
		connection = tool.Equipped:Connect(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
			local ff = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField")
			if ff and ff.Name == "SpawnFF" then
				ff:Destroy()
			end
		end)

		local clientFramework = StorageServer.Modules.Frameworks.Foods.Client:Clone()
		clientFramework.Name = tool.Name.. "-client_framework"
		clientFramework.Parent = tool
		task.wait(5)
		clientFramework:Destroy()
	end

  03:10:35.980  return function(script, AI)
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
	local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
	local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

	local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

	local Network = require(Storage.Modules.Network)
	local CastMelee = AI and require(Storage.Modules.CastMelee)

	local player = not AI and Players.LocalPlayer
	local mouse = not AI and player:GetMouse()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	local character = AI or player.Character
	if not AI then
		if not character then
			character = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
		else
			while not player.Character or not player.Character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) do
				task.wait()
			end

			task.wait(1 / 30)
		end
		character = player.Character
	end

	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

	local tool = script.Parent
	local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons:WaitForChild(tool.Name))
	local handle = tool:WaitForChild("_mod"):WaitForChild(settings.handle)

	local activateEvent
	if AI then
		activateEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
		activateEvent.Name = "activateEvent"
		activateEvent.Parent = tool	
	end

	local UI = player and player.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("UI")
	local gunInfo = UI and UI:WaitForChild("HUD_GAMEPLAY"):WaitForChild("gunInfo"):WaitForChild("main")
	local chainsawInfo = UI and UI:WaitForChild("HUD_GAMEPLAY"):WaitForChild("chainsawInfo"):WaitForChild("main")

	local function loadAnim(id)
		local animTrack = Instance.new("Animation")
		animTrack.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
		return animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack),
			animTrack:Destroy()
	end

	local data = {}
	for _,var in pairs(tool._data:GetChildren()) do
		data[var.Name] = var.Value
	end

	local anims = {}
	for animName,id in pairs(settings.anims) do
		if typeof(settings.anims[animName]) == "table" then
			anims[animName] = {}
			for animName2,id2 in pairs(settings.anims[animName]) do
				anims[animName][animName2] = loadAnim(id2)
			end
		else
			anims[animName] = loadAnim(id)
		end
	end

	local function getGlobal(name)
		return AI and character:GetAttribute(name) or _G[name]
	end

	local function setGlobal(name, value)
		if AI then
			character:SetAttribute(name, value)
		else
			_G[name] = value
		end
	end

	local reloading = false
	local active = false
	local revved = false

	local function getDistance(p1, p2)
		return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
	end

	local function getRandomChild(parent)
		return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
	end

	local function clampDistanceBetweenVectors(V1, V2, Max) -- V1 is origin.
		return V1 + ((V2 - V1).Unit * math.min((V2 - V1).Magnitude,  Max))
	end

	local equipTick = 0
	local comboTick = 0
	local rateTick = 0
	local currentAttack = 1
	local maxAttack = #anims.attack
	local equipTime = settings.chainsaw and anims.equip.Length or 0
	local delayTime = settings.chainsaw and anims.attack[(currentAttack > maxAttack and 1 or currentAttack)].Length or 0
	local delayTime2 = anims.attack[(currentAttack > maxAttack and 1 or currentAttack)].Length

	local targets = {}
	local hitbox = AI and CastMelee.new(tool)
	if hitbox then
		hitbox.Visualizer = false
		hitbox.DetectionMode = settings.chainsaw and 3 or 2
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, character}
		params.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"
		hitbox.RaycastParams = params

		hitbox.OnHit:Connect(function(hit, _, result, _, point)
			require(Storage.Modules.Library.HitboxMeleeHandler)(settings, targets, hit, humanoid, anims.attack[currentAttack], character, result, point, character, nil, tool)
		end)
	end

	local function handleMeleeHitbox(animTrack, finisher)
		local animConnections = {}
		table.insert(animConnections, animTrack:GetMarkerReachedSignal("start"):Connect(function()
			table.clear(targets)
			hitbox:HitStart()

			local soundFolder = Storage.Miscs.Weapons:FindFirstChild(tool.ToolTip) or settings.soundSource and Storage.Miscs.Weapons:FindFirstChild(settings.soundSource)
			if soundFolder and soundFolder.Sounds:FindFirstChild("Swing") and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and tool._mod:FindFirstChild(settings.handle or "Handle") then
				_G.createSound(tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].sounds.Swing, tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"], nil, nil, 0.2):Play()
			end

			if tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:FindFirstChild("Trail") then
				tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].Trail.Enabled = true
			end
		end))

		table.insert(animConnections, animTrack:GetMarkerReachedSignal("end"):Connect(function()
			hitbox:HitStop()
			if tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:FindFirstChild("Trail") then
				tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].Trail.Enabled = false
			end
		end))

		table.insert(animConnections, animTrack.Stopped:Connect(function()
			hitbox:HitStop()
			for index,event in pairs(animConnections) do
				event:Disconnect()
				animConnections[index] = nil
			end
			animConnections = nil

			setGlobal("meleeAttack", false)
			if finisher then
				setGlobal("meleeFinisher", false)
			end

			if tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"]:FindFirstChild("Trail") then
				tool._mod[settings.handle or "Handle"].Trail.Enabled = false
			end
			table.clear(targets)
		end))
	end

	local function isEquipped()
		if settings.chainsaw and equipTime <= 0 then
			equipTime = settings.chainsaw and (data.fuelCurrent > 0 and anims.equip.Length or anims.m_equip.Length) or 0
		end

		return tick() - equipTick >= (settings.chainsaw and equipTime or settings.equipTime)
	end

	local function notChainsawRev()
		return (settings.chainsaw and not anims.rev.IsPlaying and not anims.m_rev.IsPlaying) or not settings.chainsaw
	end

	local hints = {
		["H"] = anims.inspect and {info = "Inspect"} or nil,
	}

	local keyHints = {}
	local function finisherFunction(target)
		if anims.inspect then
			anims.inspect:Stop()
		end
		for _,anim in pairs(anims.attack) do
			anim:Stop()
		end
		
		if not AI then
			Network:FireServer("vars", "finish_normal", {target = target})
		end
		setGlobal("meleeFinisherAnim", true)
		setGlobal("meleeFinisher", true)
		setGlobal("isCrouch", false)
		setGlobal("isProne", false)
		anims.finisher.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
		anims.finisher:Play()
		if AI then
			handleMeleeHitbox(anims.finisher, true)
		end
		local connection
		connection = anims.finisher.Stopped:Connect(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
			setGlobal("meleeFinisherAnim", false)
		end)
		if not AI then
			UI.circularBar.Visible = true
			UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 0
			UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
			task.defer(function()								local debounce = tick()
				while anims.finisher.IsPlaying and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) do
					local progress = (anims.finisher.TimePosition / anims.finisher.Length) * 100
					if progress > 50 then
						UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
						UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = (progress / 100) * 360
					else
						UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
						UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = (progress / 50) * 180
					end

					local alpha = 0.5 + (math.abs(math.sin(tick() * 3.5) * 0.5))
					UI.circularBar.right.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), alpha)
					UI.circularBar.left.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), alpha)

					task.wait()
				end

				pcall(function()
					UI.circularBar.right.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					UI.circularBar.left.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				end)

				if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
					UI.circularBar.Visible = false
				end
			end)
		end

		humanoid.WalkSpeed = 20
		humanoid:MoveTo(clampDistanceBetweenVectors(target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position, humanoid.RootPart.Position, settings.finisherClampDist or 3))
		--[[while _G.meleeFinisher do
			if target:IsDescendantOf(workspace) and target.Humanoid.RootPart and humanoid.RootPart then
				humanoid:MoveTo(clampDistanceBetweenVectors(target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position, humanoid.RootPart.Position, 3))
			end
			task.wait(1 / 15)
		end]]
	end

	local function meleeFunctionBegan(key)
		if key == Enum.KeyCode.H and not active and tick() - rateTick >= settings.delay and humanoid.Health > 0 and not reloading and isEquipped() and anims.inspect and not anims.inspect.IsPlaying and not getGlobal("meleeFinisher") then
			for _,anim in pairs(anims.attack) do
				anim:Stop()
			end
			anims.inspect:Play()
		elseif key == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			if anims.inspect and anims.inspect.IsPlaying then
				anims.inspect:Stop()
				return
			end
			active = true

			while active do
				if tick() - rateTick >= (settings.chainsaw and delayTime or settings.delayBasedOnAnim and delayTime2 or settings.delay) and not getGlobal("carry") and humanoid.Health > 0 and isEquipped() and not getGlobal("isProne") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not getGlobal("meleeFinisher") and (settings.chainsaw and data.fuelCurrent > 0 or not settings.chainsaw) and notChainsawRev() and not ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE.revive:FindFirstChild(AI and character.Name or player.Name) then
					rateTick = tick()

					if currentAttack > maxAttack or tick() - comboTick > (settings.chainsaw and delayTime + 0.35 or settings.delayBasedOnAnim and delayTime2 + 0.2 or settings.delay + 0.2) then
						currentAttack = 1
					end

					for _,anim in pairs(anims.attack) do
						anim:Stop()
					end

					if settings.custom and settings.custom.attack_hitbox and not tool:FindFirstChild("Motor") then
						tool._mod[settings.handle].NoMotorWeld:SetAttribute("TargetPart", settings.custom.attack_hitbox[currentAttack])
					end

					local attackAnim = anims.attack[currentAttack]
					attackAnim.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
					attackAnim:Play()
					if AI then
						handleMeleeHitbox(attackAnim, false)
					end
					local lastAttack = currentAttack
					currentAttack += 1
					setGlobal("meleeAttack", true)

					comboTick = tick()
					if not AI then
						Storage.Events.client:Fire("cMeleeFire", tool, settings)
						UI.circularBar.Visible = true
						UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 0
						UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180

						task.defer(function()								
							local debounce = tick()
							while tick() - debounce < (settings.chainsaw and delayTime or settings.delayBasedOnAnim and delayTime2 or settings.delay) and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and not _G.meleeFinisher do
								local progress = ((tick() - debounce) / (settings.chainsaw and delayTime or settings.delayBasedOnAnim and delayTime2 or settings.delay)) * 100
								if progress > 50 then
									UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
									UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = (progress / 100) * 360
								else
									UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
									UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = (progress / 50) * 180
								end

								local alpha = 0.5 + (math.abs(math.sin(tick() * 3.5) * 0.5))
								UI.circularBar.right.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 115, 117):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), alpha)
								UI.circularBar.left.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 115, 117):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), alpha)

								task.wait()
							end

							if not _G.meleeFinisher then
								pcall(function()
									UI.circularBar.right.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
									UI.circularBar.left.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
								end)
							end

							if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and not _G.meleeFinisher then
								UI.circularBar.Visible = false
							end
						end)
					end


					delayTime = settings.chainsaw and anims.attack[(lastAttack > maxAttack and 1 or lastAttack)].Length + settings.delay or 0
					delayTime2 = anims.attack[(lastAttack > maxAttack and 1 or lastAttack)].Length + settings.delay
				end

				task.wait(1 / 30)
			end
		elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.R and settings.chainsaw and data.fuel > 0 and data.fuelCurrent < data.fuelSize and isEquipped() and not reloading and notChainsawRev() then
			for _,v in pairs(anims.attack) do
				v:AdjustWeight(0)
				v:Stop()
			end
			anims.finisher:Stop()

			local anim = data.fuelCurrent > 0 and anims.rev or anims.m_rev
			anim:Play()
			if not AI then
				UI.circularBar.Visible = true
				UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 0
				UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
			end
			while anim.TimePosition < anim.Length / 2 and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") do
				if not AI then
					local progress = (anim.TimePosition / (anim.Length / 2)) * 100
					if progress > 50 then
						UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
						UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = (progress / 100) * 360
					else
						UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
						UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = (progress / 50) * 180
					end

					local alpha = 0.5 + (math.abs(math.sin(tick() * 3.5) * 0.5))
					UI.circularBar.right.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), alpha)
					UI.circularBar.left.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), alpha)
				end

				task.wait()
			end

			if not AI then
				pcall(function()
					UI.circularBar.right.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					UI.circularBar.left.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				end)

				if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
					UI.circularBar.Visible = false
				end
			end

			if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") then
				if not AI and keyHints.rev then
					keyHints.rev:Destroy()
					keyHints.rev = nil
				end

				local refillSize = data.fuelCurrent > 0 and 10 or 20
				local addFuel = refillSize
				if data.fuel < refillSize then
					addFuel = data.fuel
				end	
				data.fuelCurrent = math.min(data.fuelCurrent + addFuel, data.fuelSize)
				data.fuel = math.max(data.fuel - refillSize, 0)

				anims.m_idle:Stop()
				anims.idle:Play()
			end
		end
	end

	local function meleeFunctionEnded(key)
		if key == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			active = false
		end
	end

	local connections = {}
	local function onEquipped()
		if not AI then
			if settings.chainsaw then
				UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.chainsawInfo.Visible = true
			end
			UI.circularBar.Visible = false
		end
		setGlobal("meleeEquipped", true)
		setGlobal("meleeAttack", false)
		equipTick = tick()
		if not AI then
			TweenService:Create(gunInfo.reloadProgress, TweenInfo.new(16e-5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), Transparency = 0.5, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
		end
		equipTime = settings.chainsaw and (data.fuelCurrent > 0 and not revved and anims.equip.Length or anims.m_equip.Length) or 0


		anims.idle.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
		anims.equip.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2
		if not settings.chainsaw or data.fuelCurrent > 0 then
			if not revved then
				anims.equip:Play()
				if settings.chainsaw then
					if not AI and (not keyHints.rev or not keyHints.rev:IsDescendantOf(game)) and data.fuel > 0 then
					end

					local markerConnection
					markerConnection = anims.equip:GetMarkerReachedSignal("revved"):Connect(function()
						markerConnection:Disconnect()
						markerConnection = nil
						if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
							anims.idle:Play()
						end
					end)

					local connection
					connection = anims.equip.Stopped:Connect(function()
						if markerConnection then
							markerConnection:Disconnect()
						end
						connection:Disconnect()
					end)
				else
					anims.idle:Play()
				end
			else
				anims.idle:Play()
				anims.m_equip:Play()

				if not AI and data.fuel > 0 then
					local frame = nil
					
				end
			end
		elseif settings.chainsaw then
			if not AI and data.fuel > 0 then
				local frame = nil
			
			end

			anims.m_idle.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
			anims.m_equip.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2
			anims.m_equip:Play()
			anims.m_idle:Play()
		end

		if not AI then
			table.insert(connections, Storage.Events.melee.Event:Connect(function(target)
				if not anims.finisher.IsPlaying and not getGlobal("meleeFinisher") and (settings.chainsaw and data.fuelCurrent > 0 or not settings.chainsaw) then
					finisherFunction(target)
				end
			end))

			table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
				if not process then
					meleeFunctionBegan(inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and inputObject.KeyCode or inputObject.UserInputType)
				end
			end))

			table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inputObject)
				meleeFunctionEnded(inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and inputObject.KeyCode or inputObject.UserInputType)
			end))
		else
			activateEvent.Event:Connect(function(key, mode)
				if mode == "began" then
					meleeFunctionBegan(key)
				elseif mode == "ended" then
					meleeFunctionEnded(key)
				elseif mode == "finisher" and not anims.finisher.IsPlaying and not getGlobal("meleeFinisher") and (settings.chainsaw and data.fuelCurrent > 0 or not settings.chainsaw) then
					finisherFunction(key)
					character:SetAttribute("finish", true)
					local t = tick()
					while tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.RootPart and tick() - t < 5 and character:GetAttribute("finish") do
						task.wait(1 / 15)
					end

					character:SetAttribute("finish", false)
				end
			end)
		end

		if anims.holster then
			local rateTick = 0
			local stepped = AI and RunService.Heartbeat or RunService.RenderStepped
			table.insert(connections, stepped:Connect(function()
				if tick() - rateTick >= 1 / 10 then
					if AI then
						rateTick = tick()
					end

					local linearVelocity = Vector3.new(humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.y, 0, humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.z)
					if tick() - rateTick >= (settings.chainsaw and delayTime or settings.delayBasedOnAnim and delayTime2 or settings.delay) and anims.holster and getGlobal("run") then
						anims.holster.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
						if not anims.holster.IsPlaying then
							anims.holster:Play(0.1)
						end
					elseif anims.holster and anims.holster.IsPlaying then
						anims.holster:Stop(0.25)
					end

					if anims.holster and anims.holster.IsPlaying then
						anims.idle:Stop(0.25)
					elseif not anims.idle.IsPlaying then
						anims.idle:Play(0.25)
					end

					if anims.inspect then
						setGlobal("inspect", anims.inspect.IsPlaying)
					end
				end
			end))
		end

		if settings.chainsaw then
			local drainTick = 0
			local rateTick = 0
			local stepped = AI and RunService.Heartbeat or RunService.RenderStepped
			table.insert(connections, stepped:Connect(function(dt)
				local newDt = dt * 60

				if not AI then
					chainsawInfo.itemName.Text = tostring(tool.Name) .. " []"
				end
				if isEquipped() and data then
					if not AI then
						chainsawInfo.currentFuel.Text = data.fuelCurrent .. [[<font size="25">w</font>]]
						chainsawInfo.fuelSize.Text = data.fuel
						chainsawInfo.currentFuel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 0):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), (data.fuelCurrent / data.fuelSize) * 1)
					end
					chainsawInfo.fuelSize.TextColor3 = data.fuel > 0 and Color3.fromRGB(229, 229, 229) or Color3.fromRGB(79, 0, 0)
					if revved and data.fuelCurrent > 0 and tick() - drainTick > settings.drainRate then
						drainTick = tick()
						data.fuelCurrent -= 1
					end

					if not revved and data.fuelCurrent > 0 then
						revved = true
					elseif data.fuelCurrent <= 0 and revved then
						revved = false

						if not AI then
							if not keyHints.rev or not keyHints.rev:IsDescendantOf(game) and data.fuel > 0 then
								local frame = _G.setKeyHint("R", "Rev")
							
							end
						end

						for _,v in pairs(anims.attack) do
							v:AdjustWeight(0)
							v:Stop()
						end
						anims.finisher:Stop()

						anims.equip:Stop()
						anims.idle:Stop()
						anims.m_idle:Play()

						anims.m_state.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
						anims.m_state:Play()
						equipTime = anims.m_equip.Length
					end
				else
					chainsawInfo.currentFuel.Text = "-"
					chainsawInfo.fuelSize.Text = "-"
				end
			end))
		--[[else
			table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if _G.carry and anims.idle.IsPlaying then
					anims.idle:Stop()
				elseif not _G.carry and not anims.idle.IsPlaying then
					anims.idle:Play()
				end
			end))]]
		end
	end
	if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
		onEquipped()
	end

	tool.Equipped:Connect(onEquipped)
	tool.Unequipped:Connect(function()
		active = false

		setGlobal("inspect", false)
		setGlobal("meleeEquipped", false)
		if not AI then
			UI.circularBar.Visible = false
			UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.chainsawInfo.Visible = false
		end

		for i,v in pairs(connections) do
			v:Disconnect()
			connections[i] = nil
		end

		if not AI then
			for index,frame in pairs(keyHints) do
				if frame:IsDescendantOf(game) then
					_G.clearKeyHint(frame)
				end

				keyHints[index] = nil
			end
			keyHints = {}

			TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(16e-5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {FieldOfView = 70}):Play()
		end

		for index,v in pairs(anims) do
			if typeof(anims[index]) == "table" then
				for _,k in pairs(anims[index]) do
					k:AdjustWeight(0)
					k:Stop()
				end
			else
				v:AdjustWeight(0)
				v:Stop()
			end
		end
	end)
	
	local connection
	connection = tool.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
		if parent == nil then
			connection:Disconnect()

			for index,v in pairs(anims) do
				if typeof(anims[index]) == "table" then
					for _,k in pairs(anims[index]) do
						k:AdjustWeight(0)
						k:Stop()
					end
				else
					v:AdjustWeight(0)
					v:Stop()
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end  -  Editar

  03:10:35.980  return function(script)
	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")

	local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
	local StorageServer = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

	local tool = script.Parent
	local player = tool.Parent.Parent

	local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons:WaitForChild(tool.Name))

	local character = player:IsA("Player") and (player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()) or tool.Parent
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

	if tool:FindFirstChild("Motor") then
		tool.Motor.Part0 = character:WaitForChild("Right Arm")
	else
		tool._mod[settings.handle].NoMotorWeld.Part0 = character:WaitForChild("Right Arm")
	end
	
	for _,v in pairs(tool._data:GetChildren()) do
		if settings[v.Name] then
			v.Value = settings[v.Name]
		end
	end
	
	for _,part in pairs(tool._mod[settings.handle]:GetChildren()) do
		if part:IsA("BasePart") then
			local weld = part:FindFirstChild("WeldWithPartName")
			if weld and character:FindFirstChild(part.Name) then
				weld.Part0 = character[part.Name]
			end
		end
	end
	
	if player:IsA("Player") then
		local connection
		
		connection = tool.Equipped:Connect(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
			local ff = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField")
			
			if ff and ff.Name == "SpawnFF" then
				ff:Destroy()
			end
		end)

		local clientFramework = StorageServer.Modules.Frameworks.Melee.Client:Clone()
		clientFramework.Name = tool.Name.. "-client_framework"
		clientFramework.Parent = tool
		task.wait(5)
		clientFramework:Destroy()
	else
		require(StorageServer.Modules.Frameworks.Melee.Client)(script, character)
	end
end  -  Editar

  03:10:35.981  PATH: ServerStorage.Storage.Modules.Frameworks.Grenades.Client  -  Editar

  03:10:35.981  ------------------------------------------  -  Editar
  03:10:35.981  return function(script)
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
	local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
	local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
	local CollectionService = game:GetService("CollectionService")

	local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

	local Network = require(Storage.Modules.Network)
	
	local player = Players.LocalPlayer
	local mouse = player:GetMouse()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	local character = player.Character
	if not character then
		character = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
	else
		while not player.Character or not player.Character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) do
			task.wait()
		end
		
		task.wait(1 / 30)
	end
	character = player.Character
	
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

	local tool = script.Parent
	local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons:WaitForChild(tool.Name))
	local handle = tool:WaitForChild("_mod"):WaitForChild(settings.handle)
	
	local Trajectory = require(Storage.Modules.Trajectory).new(Vector3.new(0, -game.Workspace.Gravity, 0), {MaxBounce = settings.maxBounce})
	
	local UI = player.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("UI")
	local gunInfo = UI:WaitForChild("HUD_GAMEPLAY"):WaitForChild("gunInfo"):WaitForChild("main")
	local chainsawInfo = UI:WaitForChild("HUD_GAMEPLAY"):WaitForChild("chainsawInfo"):WaitForChild("main")
	
	local function loadAnim(id)
		local animTrack = Instance.new("Animation")
		animTrack.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
		return animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack),
			animTrack:Destroy()
	end

	local data = {}
	for _,var in pairs(tool._data:GetChildren()) do
		data[var.Name] = var.Value
	end

	local anims = {}
	for animName,id in pairs(settings.anims) do
		if typeof(settings.anims[animName]) == "table" then
			anims[animName] = {}
			for animName2,id2 in pairs(settings.anims[animName]) do
				anims[animName][animName2] = loadAnim(id2)
			end
		else
			anims[animName] = loadAnim(id)
		end
	end

	local active = false
	local used = false

	local function getDistance(p1, p2)
		return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
	end

	local function getRandomChild(parent)
		return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
	end
	
	local function clampDistanceBetweenVectors(V1, V2, Max) -- V1 is origin.
		return V1 + ((V2 - V1).Unit * math.min((V2 - V1).Magnitude,  Max))
	end
	
	local equipTick = 0
	local rateTick = 0
	local equipTime = 0
	
	local function isEquipped()
		return tick() - equipTick >= settings.equipTime
	end
	
	local hints = {
		["H"] = anims.inspect and {info = "Inspect"} or nil,
	}
	
	local trajectoryConnection, components
	local trajectorHitPoint = Storage.Models.trajectoryPoint:Clone()
	CollectionService:AddTag(trajectorHitPoint, "_Trajectory")
	local function clearTrajectory(disconnect)
		if components then
			for _,v in pairs(components) do
				v:Destroy()
			end
			table.clear(components)
			components = nil
		end
		
		if disconnect and trajectoryConnection then
			trajectorHitPoint.Parent = nil
			trajectoryConnection:Disconnect()
			trajectoryConnection = nil
		end
	end
	
	local function isHealthy()
		return not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Carried") and humanoid.Health > 0
	end

	local keyHints = {}
	local connections = {}
	local function onEquipped()
		if used then
			humanoid:UnequipTools()
			return
		end
		
		_G.grenadeEquipped = true
		anims.cancel:Play()
		for _,partName in pairs(settings.simulatePhysicsParts) do
			CollectionService:AddTag(tool._mod[partName], "SmartBone")
		end
		UI.circularBar.Visible = false
		equipTick = tick()
		TweenService:Create(gunInfo.reloadProgress, TweenInfo.new(16e-5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), Transparency = 0.5, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
		equipTime = 0
		

		
		anims.idle.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
		anims.equip.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2
		anims.equip:Play()
		anims.idle:Play()
		
		table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
			if not process then
				if inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.H and not active and tick() - rateTick >= settings.delay and humanoid.Health > 0 and isEquipped() and anims.inspect and not anims.inspect.IsPlaying and not anims.use.IsPlaying and not active then
					anims.inspect:Play()
				elseif inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and isHealthy() then
					if anims.inspect and anims.inspect.IsPlaying then
						anims.inspect:Stop()
						return
					end
					
					if isEquipped() and not used then
						if not active then
							if anims.use.IsPlaying then
								return
							end
							
							Storage.Events.client:Fire("cMeleeFire", tool, settings)
							anims.throw_idle:Play()
							anims.use.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
							anims.use:Play(0.0000001)
							_G.grenadeUse = true
							_G.grenadeEquipped = true
							
							trajectoryConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
								_G.aim = true
								if not anims.use.IsPlaying and isHealthy() then
									active = true
									clearTrajectory()
									
									if not trajectorHitPoint:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
										trajectorHitPoint.Parent = workspace.Ignored
									end

									local dir = (mouse.Hit.p - tool._mod[settings.handle].Position).unit
									local x0 = tool._mod[settings.handle].Position + dir * 2
									local v0 = dir * settings.speed + Vector3.new(0, settings.upForce, 0)

									local path = Trajectory:Cast(x0, v0, Enum.Material.Plastic, {workspace.Ignored, camera})
									local point
									components, point = Trajectory:Draw(path)
									trajectorHitPoint.CFrame = point and CFrame.new(point[1], point[1] + point[2]) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0) or CFrame.new(10e5, 10e5, 10e5)
								end
							end)
						elseif active and not used then
							anims.use:Stop()
							anims.throw_idle:Stop()
							clearTrajectory(true)
							_G.aim = false
							_G.grenadeUse = false
							_G.grenadeEquipped = false

							if isHealthy() then
								active = false
								used = true
								anims.idle:Stop()
								--_G.fireActive = tick()
								anims.throw:Play()
								Storage.Events.client:Fire("cMeleeFire", tool, settings)

								local dir = (mouse.Hit.p - tool._mod[settings.handle].Position).unit
								Network:FireServer("grenade", tool, tool._mod[settings.handle].Position, dir)
							end

							anims.cancel:Play()
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end))
		
		if anims.holster then
			table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				local linearVelocity = Vector3.new(humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.y, 0, humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.z)
				if tick() - rateTick >= settings.delay and anims.holster and _G.run then
					anims.holster.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
					if not anims.holster.IsPlaying then
						anims.holster:Play(0.1)
					end
				elseif anims.holster and anims.holster.IsPlaying then
					anims.holster:Stop(0.25)
				end

				if anims.holster and anims.holster.IsPlaying then
					anims.idle:Stop(0.25)
				elseif not anims.idle.IsPlaying then
					anims.idle:Play(0.25)
				end
				
				if anims.inspect then
					_G.inspect = anims.inspect.IsPlaying
				end
			end))
		end
	end
	if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
		onEquipped()
	end
	
	tool.Equipped:Connect(onEquipped)
	tool.Unequipped:Connect(function()
		if active or anims.use.IsPlaying then
			local dir = (tool._mod[settings.handle].Position + Vector3.new(0, -100, 0)).unit
			Network:FireServer("grenade", tool, tool._mod[settings.handle].Position, dir)
		end
		active = false
		
		_G.grenadeEquipped = false
		_G.aim = false
		_G.grenadeUse = false
		_G.inspect = false
		UI.circularBar.Visible = false
		UI.HUD_GAMEPLAY.chainsawInfo.Visible = false
		clearTrajectory(true)

		for i,v in pairs(connections) do
			v:Disconnect()
			connections[i] = nil
		end
		
		for index,frame in pairs(keyHints) do
			if frame:IsDescendantOf(game) then
				_G.clearKeyHint(frame)
			end

			keyHints[index] = nil
		end
		keyHints = {}

		TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(16e-5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {FieldOfView = 70}):Play()

		for index,v in pairs(anims) do
			if typeof(anims[index]) == "table" then
				for _,k in pairs(anims[index]) do
					k:AdjustWeight(0)
					k:Stop()
				end
			else
				v:AdjustWeight(0)
				v:Stop()
			end
		end
	end)
	
	local connection
	connection = tool.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
		if parent == nil then
			connection:Disconnect()
			
			for index,v in pairs(anims) do
				if typeof(anims[index]) == "table" then
					for _,k in pairs(anims[index]) do
						k:AdjustWeight(0)
						k:Stop()
					end
				else
					v:AdjustWeight(0)
					v:Stop()
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end  -  Editar

  03:10:35.981  return function(script)
	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")

	local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
	local StorageServer = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

	local tool = script.Parent
	local player = tool.Parent.Parent

	local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons:WaitForChild(tool.Name))

	local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

	if tool:FindFirstChild("Motor") then
		tool.Motor.Part0 = character:WaitForChild("Right Arm")
	else
		tool._mod[settings.handle].NoMotorWeld.Part0 = character:WaitForChild("Right Arm")
	end
	for _,v in pairs(tool._data:GetChildren()) do
		if settings[v.Name] then
			v.Value = settings[v.Name]
		end
	end
	for _,part in pairs(tool._mod[settings.handle]:GetChildren()) do
		if part:IsA("BasePart") then
			local weld = part:FindFirstChild("WeldWithPartName")
			if weld and character:FindFirstChild(part.Name) then
				weld.Part0 = character[part.Name]
			end
		end
	end
	
	local connection
	connection = tool.Equipped:Connect(function()
		connection:Disconnect()
		local ff = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField")
		if ff and ff.Name == "SpawnFF" then
			ff:Destroy()
		end
	end)
	
	local clientFramework = StorageServer.Modules.Frameworks.Grenades.Client:Clone()
	clientFramework.Name = tool.Name.. "-client_framework"
	clientFramework.Parent = tool
	task.wait(5)
	clientFramework:Destroy()
end  -  Editar
