  03:10:35.982  return function(script, AI)
	local Players = game:GetService("Players")
	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
	local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
	local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

	local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

	local Network = require(Storage.Modules.Network)

	local player = not AI and Players.LocalPlayer
	local mouse = not AI and player:GetMouse()
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

	local character = AI or player.Character
	if not AI then
		if not character then
			character = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
		else
			while not player.Character or not player.Character:IsDescendantOf(workspace) do
				task.wait()
			end

			task.wait(1 / 30)
		end
		character = player.Character
	end

	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
	local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

	local tool = script.Parent
	local handle = tool:WaitForChild("_mod"):WaitForChild("Handle")

	local activateEvent
	if AI then
		activateEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
		activateEvent.Name = "activateEvent"
		activateEvent.Parent = tool	
	end

	local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons:WaitForChild(tool.Name))

	local UI = player and player.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("UI")
	local gunInfo = UI and UI:WaitForChild("HUD_GAMEPLAY"):WaitForChild("gunInfo"):WaitForChild("main")

	local function loadAnim(id)
		local animTrack = Instance.new("Animation")
		animTrack.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
		return animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack),
		animTrack:Destroy()
	end

	local data = {}
	for _,var in pairs(tool._data:GetChildren()) do
		data[var.Name] = var.Value
	end

	local anims = {}
	for animName,id in pairs(settings.anims) do
		anims[animName] = loadAnim(id)
	end

	local function getGlobal(name)
		return AI and character:GetAttribute(name) or _G[name]
	end

	local function setGlobal(name, value)
		if AI then
			character:SetAttribute(name, value)
		else
			_G[name] = value
		end
	end

	local reloading = false
	local active = false
	local fireProcess = false
	local isActuallyAiming = false
	setGlobal("reload", false)

	local function getDistance(p1, p2)
		return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
	end

	local function getRandomChild(parent)
		return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
	end

	local equipTick = 0
	local rateTick = 0
	local reloadFinishTime
	local walkSpeed = 0

	local fireMode = settings.mode
	local currentFireMode = 1
	local fireModeDelay = 0

	local function isEquipped()
		return tick() - equipTick >= settings.equipTime
	end

	local function isHumanoid(part)
		if part.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
			return part.Parent
		elseif part.Parent ~= workspace then
			return isHumanoid(part.Parent)
		end

		return false
	end

	local gunParams = RaycastParams.new()
	gunParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, camera, character}
	gunParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	gunParams.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"

	local function getHitPos(muzzle)
		local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local cast = workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, mouse.UnitRay.Direction.unit * 10000, gunParams)
		local origin = head and head.Position or muzzle.WorldPosition
		if cast then
			return origin, (cast.Position - origin).unit
		end

		return origin, mouse.UnitRay.Direction
	end

	local function sightBlocked(muzzle)
		local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
		local cast = workspace:Raycast(camera.CFrame.Position, mouse.UnitRay.Direction.unit * 10000, gunParams)
		local origin = head and head.Position or muzzle.WorldPosition
		if cast then
			local cast2 = workspace:Raycast(origin, (cast.Position + cast.Normal) - origin, gunParams)
			if cast2 and getDistance(origin, cast2.Position) < 50 and not isHumanoid(cast2.Instance) then
				return true, cast2.Position
			end
		end

		return false
	end

	humanoid.Running:Connect(function(speed)
		if anims.holster then
			if getGlobal("run") then
				anims.holster:AdjustSpeed(speed / 10)
			else
				anims.holster:AdjustSpeed(0)
			end
		end

		walkSpeed = speed
	end)

	local hints = {
		["T"] = anims.shove and {info = "Shove"} or nil,
		["H"] = anims.inspect and {info = "Inspect"} or nil,
		["B"] = settings.mode_switch and {info = "Switch Mode"} or nil,
	}

	--local keyHints = {}
	local function gunFunctionBegan(key)
		if key == Enum.KeyCode.R and not settings.noReload and not reloading and data.magCurrent < data.magSize and data.ammo > 0 and isEquipped() then
			reloading = true
			setGlobal("reload", true)
			while (settings.shotgun and anims.load.Length <= 0 or not settings.shotgun and anims.reload.Length <= 0) and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) do
				task.wait()
			end
			if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
				anims.fire.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
				anims.fire:Play()
				if not getGlobal("aim") then
					anims.fire:AdjustWeight(1)
				end
				anims.fire:Stop()
				if anims.inspect then
					anims.inspect:Stop(0.00000001)
				end
				if anims.inspect_empty then
					anims.inspect_empty:Stop(0.00000001)
				end
				if anims.fanfire then
					anims.fanfire:Stop()
				end

				if not AI then
					gunInfo.reloadProgress.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
					gunInfo.reloadProgress.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
					gunInfo.reloadProgress.Transparency = 0.5

					UI.circularBar.Visible = true
					UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 0
					UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180

					task.spawn(function()
						while reloading do
							local alpha = 0.5 + (math.abs(math.sin(tick() * 3.5) * 0.5))
							UI.circularBar.right.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), alpha)
							UI.circularBar.left.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), alpha)
							task.wait()
						end

						pcall(function()
							UI.circularBar.right.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
							UI.circularBar.left.image.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
						end)
					end)
				end

				if settings.shotgun then
					if not AI then
						Network:FireServer("vars", "reload", {tool = tool, state = true})
					end

					if data.magCurrent <= 0 then
						anims.preload_empty.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3
						anims.preload_empty:Play()
						task.wait(anims.preload_empty.Length / 2)
						if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and reloading then
							if not AI then
								Network:FireServer("vars", "reload", {tool = tool, add = true})
							end
							data.magCurrent += 1
							data.ammo = math.max(data.ammo - 1, 0)
							if AI then
								tool._data.magCurrent.Value = data.magCurrent
								tool._data.ammo.Value = data.ammo
							end
						end
						task.wait((anims.preload_empty.Length / 2) - 0.1)
					else
						anims.preload.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3
						anims.preload:Play()
						task.wait(anims.preload.Length - 0.1)
					end

					if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and reloading and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and humanoid.Health > 0 and data.magCurrent < data.magSize and data.ammo > 0 then
						anims.load.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3
						if not AI then
							task.spawn(function()
								while reloading and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) do
									local progress = (anims.load.TimePosition / anims.load.Length) * 100
									if progress > 50 then
										UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
										--UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = (progress / 100) * 360
										TweenService:Create(UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient, TweenInfo.new(1 / 15), {Rotation = (progress / 100) * 360}):Play()
									else
										UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
										--UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = (progress / 50) * 180
										TweenService:Create(UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient, TweenInfo.new(1 / 15), {Rotation = (progress / 50) * 180}):Play()
									end

									task.wait(1 / 15)
								end
							end)
						end

						local isReloaded = false
						while tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and reloading and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and humanoid.Health > 0 and (AI or not UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)) and data.magCurrent < data.magSize and data.ammo > 0 do
							anims.load:Play()
							--TweenService:Create(gunInfo.reloadProgress, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Size = UDim2.new((anims.reload.TimePosition / maxTime) * 1, 0, 1, 0), Transparency = 0.5, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
							while true do
								if anims.load.TimePosition >= anims.load.Length then
									isReloaded = true
									break
								end

								if not anims.load.IsPlaying then
									break
								end

								task.wait()
							end

							if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and reloading and isReloaded then
								if not AI then
									Network:FireServer("vars", "reload", {tool = tool, add = true})
								end
								data.magCurrent += 1
								data.ammo = math.max(data.ammo - 1, 0)
								if AI then
									tool._data.magCurrent.Value = data.magCurrent
									tool._data.ammo.Value = data.ammo
								end
							end

							if data.magCurrent >= data.magSize then
								break
							end
						end

						if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) or not character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool") then
							UI.circularBar.Visible = false
						end
					end

					if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
						if not AI then
							Network:FireServer("vars", "reload", {tool = tool, client = true})
						end
						anims.afterload.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3
						anims.afterload:Play()
					end
				else
					anims.reload.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3
					anims.reload:Play()
					local getTime
					getTime = anims.reload:GetMarkerReachedSignal(settings.tacReloadMarker and "tacReload" or "clipIn"):Connect(function()
						if not reloadFinishTime and not settings.noTacReload then
							reloadFinishTime = anims.reload.TimePosition + settings.tacReloadAdditional
						end

						if anims.noammo then
							anims.noammo:Stop()
						end
					end)
					if not AI then
						Network:FireServer("vars", "reload", {tool = tool, state = true})
					end

					local isReloaded = false
					while tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and reloading and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and humanoid.Health > 0 do
						local maxTime = data.magCurrent > 0 and reloadFinishTime or anims.reload.Length
						if anims.reload.TimePosition >= anims.reload.Length or (reloadFinishTime and anims.reload.TimePosition >= reloadFinishTime and data.magCurrent > 0) then
							isReloaded = true
							--[[local needAmmo = data.magSize - data.magCurrent
							data.magCurrent = math.min(data.magCurrent + data.ammo, data.magSize)
							data.ammo = math.max(data.ammo - needAmmo, 0)]]
							data.magCurrent = data.magSize
							data.ammo = math.max(data.ammo - 1, 0)
							if AI then
								tool._data.magCurrent.Value = data.magCurrent
								tool._data.ammo.Value = data.ammo
							end
							if not AI then
								Network:FireServer("vars", "reload", {tool = tool, client = true})
							end
							anims.reload:Stop(0.25)
							--TweenService:Create(gunInfo.reloadProgress, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 255, 127), Transparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()

							break
						end

						if not AI then
							local progress = (anims.reload.TimePosition / maxTime) * 100
							if progress > 50 then
								UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
								--UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = (progress / 100) * 360
								TweenService:Create(UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient, TweenInfo.new(1 / 15), {Rotation = (progress / 100) * 360}):Play()
							else
								UI.circularBar.left.image.UIGradient.Rotation = 180
								--UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient.Rotation = (progress / 50) * 180
								TweenService:Create(UI.circularBar.right.image.UIGradient, TweenInfo.new(1 / 15), {Rotation = (progress / 50) * 180}):Play()
							end
						end

						--TweenService:Create(gunInfo.reloadProgress, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Size = UDim2.new((anims.reload.TimePosition / maxTime) * 1, 0, 1, 0), Transparency = 0.5, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
						task.wait(1 / 15)
					end

					if (tool:IsDescendantOf(character) or not character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")) and not AI then
						UI.circularBar.Visible = false
					end

					if getTime then
						getTime:Disconnect()
					end

					if not isReloaded and anims.noammo and data.magCurrent <= 0 then
						anims.noammo:Play()
					end

					anims.reload:Stop()
				end
				--[[for name,data in pairs(data) do
					print(tostring(name) .. ": [c]:" .. tostring(data) .. " [s]:" .. tostring(tool._data:FindFirstChild(name) and tool._data[name].Value or "-"))
				end]]
			end

			reloading = false
			setGlobal("reload", false)
		elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.H and not active and not fireProcess and tick() - rateTick >= settings.rate and humanoid.Health > 0 and not reloading and isEquipped() and anims.inspect and not anims.inspect.IsPlaying and (anims.inspect_empty and not anims.inspect_empty.IsPlaying or not anims.inspect_empty) then
			anims.fire.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
			if data.magCurrent > 0 or not anims.inspect_empty then
				anims.inspect:Play()
			else
				anims.inspect_empty:Play()
				if anims.noammo then
					anims.noammo:Stop()

					local connection
					connection = anims.inspect_empty.Stopped:Connect(function()
						anims.noammo:Play(0.0000001)
					end)
				end
			end
		elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.B and not active and not fireProcess and tick() - rateTick >= settings.rate and humanoid.Health > 0 and not reloading and isEquipped() and settings.mode_switch and tick() - fireModeDelay >= 0.5 then
			fireModeDelay = tick()
			currentFireMode += 1
			if currentFireMode > #settings.mode_switch then
				currentFireMode = 1
			end
			fireMode = settings.mode_switch[currentFireMode]
			_G.changeFireMode()
			_G.createSound(player.PlayerGui.SOUNDS.fireSwitch, player.PlayerGui):Play()
		elseif key == Enum.KeyCode.T and not active and not fireProcess and humanoid.Health > 0 and not reloading and isEquipped() and anims.shove and tick() - rateTick >= settings.rate_shove then
			rateTick = tick()
			setGlobal("fireActive", tick())
			anims.shove:Play()
		elseif key == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and not active and not fireProcess and tick() - rateTick >= settings.rate and humanoid.Health > 0 and isEquipped() and not _G.unableToShoot and not ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE["self-revive"]:FindFirstChild(AI and character.Name or player.Name) then
			if anims.inspect and anims.inspect.IsPlaying then
				anims.inspect:Stop()
				if settings.gunType == "diy_ft" then
					anims.light:Play()
				end

				return
			end

			if anims.inspect_empty and anims.inspect_empty.IsPlaying then
				anims.inspect_empty:Stop()
				return
			end

			if not reloading then
				active = true
				local fanfire = false
				if settings.prefireDelay then
					anims.prefire:Play()
					local downed = tick()
					local prefired = false
					while active and tool:IsDescendantOf(character) do
						if tick() - downed >= settings.prefireDelay then
							prefired = true
							break
						end

						task.wait()
					end

					if not tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
						anims.prefire:Stop()
						return
					elseif not prefired and anims.fanfire and (not getGlobal("aim") or not AI and not _G.isFPS()) then
						fanfire = true
						anims.prefire:Stop()
						anims.fanfire:Play()
					elseif not prefired then
						anims.prefire:Stop()
						return
					end
				end

				fireProcess = true
				if data.magCurrent > 0 and (settings.shotgun and not anims.afterload.IsPlaying or not settings.shotgun and (not anims.reload or not anims.reload.IsPlaying)) then
					--[[if settings.mode == 1 then
						fire()
					elseif settings.mode == 2 then
						fire()

						local deltaT = (settings.rate / 2) / 1
						local accumulator = 0
						local frameTime = 0

						local killLoop = false
						local count = 0
						while not killLoop and count < settings.amountPerRound - 1 do
							accumulator = accumulator + frameTime
							while accumulator >= deltaT do
								if not fire() then
									killLoop = true
									break
								end
								count += 1
								accumulator = accumulator - deltaT
							end

							if killLoop then
								break
							end

							frameTime = RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
						end
					elseif settings.mode == 3 then
						fire()

						local deltaT = settings.rate / 1
						local accumulator = 0
						local frameTime = 0

						local killLoop = false
						while tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and active and not reloading and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not killLoop do
							accumulator = accumulator + frameTime
							while accumulator >= deltaT do
								if not fire() then
									killLoop = true
									break
								end
								accumulator = accumulator - deltaT
							end

							frameTime = RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
							--task.wait(settings.rate)
						end
					end]]

					local rate = 0
					local count = 0
					local wasFired = false
					local lastAmmoAmount = data.magCurrent
					local diyFTfire = false
					if settings.gunType == "diy_ft" then
						anims.after:Stop()
						anims.shake:Play()
					end
					while tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and (active or fanfire) and not reloading and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Ragdoll") and not getGlobal("unableToShoot") and not ReplicatedStorage.__tempSTORAGE["self-revive"]:FindFirstChild(AI and character.Name or player.Name) do
						if data.magCurrent > 0 and settings.gunType == "diy_ft" and not anims.shake.IsPlaying and not diyFTfire then
							anims.fire_start:Play()
							diyFTfire = true
						end

						if tick() - rate >= settings.rate and (settings.gunType ~= "diy_ft" or diyFTfire) then
							rate = tick()
							if tool._data.reload.Value == true then
								reloading = false
								Network:FireServer("vars", "reload", {tool = tool, state = false})
							end

							local function fire()
								if data.magCurrent > 0 then
									if tool._mod.Handle.MuzzleFX:FindFirstChild("SmokeHeat") then
										tool._mod.Handle.MuzzleFX.SmokeHeat.Enabled = false
									end
									wasFired = true
									rateTick = tick()
									data.magCurrent = math.max(data.magCurrent - 1, 0)
									anims.fire.Priority = not fanfire and Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4 or Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
									if settings.gunType ~= "diy_ft" or not anims.fire.IsPlaying then
										anims.fire:Play()
									end
									if not getGlobal("aim") and not AI then
										anims.fire:AdjustWeight(1)
									end
									setGlobal("fireActive", tick())
									--camera.FieldOfView += settings.fireFov
									--TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {FieldOfView = 70}):Play()
									if not AI then
										Storage.Events.client:Fire("cGunFire", tool, settings)

										local blur = Instance.new("DepthOfFieldEffect")
										blur.FarIntensity = 0
										blur.InFocusRadius = _G.isFPS() and _G.aim and 48.9 or 45
										blur.Parent = camera
										Debris:AddItem(blur, 0.15)
										TweenService:create(blur, TweenInfo.new(0.5), {InFocusRadius = 50}):Play()

										local origin, dir = getHitPos(handle.MuzzleFX)
										Storage.Events.client:Fire("fire", tool.Name, origin, dir, character, handle)
										Network:FireServer("fire", tool, dir)
									else
										Storage.Events.server:Fire("fire", character, tool, false, tool:GetAttribute("AimDir"))

										local params = RaycastParams.new()
										params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Ignored, camera, character}
										params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
										params.IgnoreWater = true
										params.CollisionGroup = "Raycast"
										local cast = workspace:Raycast(character.Head.Position, tool:GetAttribute("AimDir"), params)
										if cast then
											local target = isHumanoid(cast.Instance)
											if target then
												Storage.Events.server:Fire("damage", character, target, tool, cast.Instance, tool:GetAttribute("AimDir"), {
													Instance = cast.Instance,
													Position = cast.Position,
													Normal = cast.Normal
												}, tool:GetAttribute("AimDir"), tool:GetAttribute("AimDir"))
											end
										end
									end
									count += 1

									if anims.noammo and data.magCurrent <= 0 then
										anims.fire.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2
										anims.noammo:Play()
										return false
									end
								else
									--anims.fire.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
									--if not AI then
									--if (not keyHints.reload or not keyHints.reload:IsDescendantOf(game)) and data.ammo > 0 and not settings.noReloadHint then
									--local frame = _G.setKeyHint("R", "Reload", 5)
									--keyHints.reload = frame
									--end
									--end
									if anims.noammo then
										anims.noammo:Play()
									end
									if handle.MuzzleFX:FindFirstChild("Empty") then
										handle.MuzzleFX.Empty:Play()
									end

									return false
								end

								return true
							end

							if fireMode == 2 then
								for k = 1, settings.burstAmount do
									local fired = fire()
									if not fired then
										break
									end

									task.wait(settings.rate * 0.9)
								end

								task.wait(settings.rate * 0.8)
								break
							else
								local fired = fire()
								if not fired then
									break
								end
							end

							if fireMode == 1 then
								break
							end
						end

						task.wait()
					end
					if wasFired and tool._mod.Handle.MuzzleFX:FindFirstChild("SmokeHeat") then
						tool._mod.Handle.MuzzleFX.SmokeHeat.Enabled = true
						local t = tick()
						task.spawn(function()
							while tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and tool._mod.Handle.MuzzleFX.SmokeHeat.Enabled do
								if tick() - t >= math.min((lastAmmoAmount - data.magCurrent) / 1.5, 5) then
									tool._mod.Handle.MuzzleFX.SmokeHeat.Enabled = false
									break
								end
								task.wait()
							end
						end)
					end
					if settings.gunType == "diy_ft" then
						anims.shake:Stop()
						anims.fire_start:Stop()
						anims.fire:Stop()
						anims.cancel:Play()
						anims.after:Play()
					end
							--[[elseif anims.reload.IsPlaying then
								anims.reload:Stop(0.25)]]
				else
					if not AI then
						--if (not keyHints.reload or not keyHints.reload:IsDescendantOf(game)) and data.ammo > 0 and not settings.noReloadHint then
						--local frame = _G.setKeyHint("R", "Reload", 5)
						--keyHints.reload = frame
						--end

						_G.messageLog({
							Text = "OUT OF AMMO [" .. tool.Name .. "]",
							TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 94, 97),
						}, false, 2)
					end

					anims.fire.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
					if anims.noammo then
						anims.noammo:Play()
					end
					handle.MuzzleFX.Empty:Play()
					if anims.fire.WeightCurrent < 0.1 then
						anims.fire:Play()
					end
					if not AI then
						anims.fire:AdjustWeight(0.01)
					end
					if settings.prefireDelay then
						anims.prefire:Stop()
					end
				end

				fireProcess = false
			else
				if not AI then
					TweenService:Create(gunInfo.reloadProgress, TweenInfo.new(0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0), Transparency = 1, Size = gunInfo.reloadProgress.Size}):Play()
				end
				if not settings.shotgun then
					anims.reload:Stop(0.25)
				else
					anims.load:Stop(0.25)
				end
				reloading = false
				setGlobal("reload", false)
				if not AI then
					Network:FireServer("vars", "reload", {tool = tool, state = false})
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function gunFunctionEnded(key)
		if key == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
			active = false
		elseif key == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 and data.magCurrent > 0 then
			if anims.inspect then
				anims.inspect:Stop()
				if settings.gunType == "diy_ft" then
					anims.light:Play()
				end
			end

			if anims.inspect_empty then
				anims.inspect_empty:Stop()
			end
			anims.fire:AdjustWeight(1)
		end
	end

	local aimCrosshair = _G.aim
	local connections = {}
	local function onEquipped()
		if not AI then
			UI.circularBar.Visible = false
		end
		setGlobal("gunEquipped", true)
		equipTick = tick()

		--if not AI then
		--for key,data in pairs(hints) do
		--local frame = _G.setKeyHint(key, data.info, 5)
		--keyHints[#keyHints + 1] = frame
		--end
		--TweenService:Create(gunInfo.reloadProgress, TweenInfo.new(16e-5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0), Transparency = 0.5, BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
		--end

		anims.idle.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
		anims.equip.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2
		anims.fire.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Movement
		if anims.inspect then
			anims.inspect.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3
		end
		if anims.equip_empty then
			anims.equip_empty.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2
		end

		if anims.inspect_empty then
			anims.inspect_empty.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action3
		end

		if data.magCurrent > 0 or not anims.equip_empty then
			anims.equip:Play()
		else
			anims.equip_empty:Play()
		end
		anims.idle:Play()
		if anims.noammo and data.magCurrent <= 0 then
			anims.noammo:Play()
		end

		--[[for animName,markers in pairs(settings.markers) do
			for markerName,soundName in pairs(markers) do
				table.insert(connections, anims[animName]:GetMarkerReachedSignal(markerName):Connect(function()
					handle.sounds[soundName]:Play()
				end))
			end
		end]]

		if not AI then
			table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
				if not process then
					gunFunctionBegan(inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and inputObject.KeyCode or inputObject.UserInputType)
				end
			end))

			table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inputObject)
				gunFunctionEnded(inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and inputObject.KeyCode or inputObject.UserInputType)
			end))
		else
			activateEvent.Event:Connect(function(key, mode)
				if mode == "began" then
					gunFunctionBegan(key)
				elseif mode == "ended" then
					gunFunctionEnded(key)
				end
			end)
		end

		local rateTick = 0
		local stepped = AI and RunService.Heartbeat or RunService.RenderStepped
		table.insert(connections, stepped:Connect(function()
			if not tool:IsDescendantOf(character) or not humanoid.RootPart then
				return
			end

			if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and humanoid.RootPart then
				if tick() - rateTick >= 1 / 10 then
					if AI then
						rateTick = tick()
					end

					local linearVelocity = Vector3.new(humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.y, 0, humanoid.RootPart.Velocity.z)
					if anims.holster then
						if not getGlobal("aim") and tick() - getGlobal("fireActive") >= 0.5 and (AI or not _G.isFPS()) and not anims.equip.IsPlaying and not anims.fire.IsPlaying and not getGlobal("isProne") and not getGlobal("isCrouch") then
							if anims.holsterIdle then
								anims.holsterIdle.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action2
								if not anims.holsterIdle.IsPlaying then
									anims.holsterIdle:Play(0.2)
								end
							end

							anims.holster.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
							if not anims.holster.IsPlaying then
								anims.holster:Play(0.1)
								anims.holster:AdjustSpeed(0)
							end
						elseif (AI or _G.isFPS()) and getGlobal("run") and not anims.fire.IsPlaying and not anims.equip.IsPlaying and not getGlobal("isProne") and not getGlobal("isCrouch") then
							if anims.holsterIdle then
								anims.holsterIdle:Stop()
							end
							anims.holster.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
							if not anims.holster.IsPlaying then
								anims.fire.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
								anims.holster:Play(0.15)
							end
						else
							if getGlobal("aim") and (AI or _G.isFPS()) then
								if not anims.holster.IsPlaying then
									anims.holster:Play()
								end
								anims.holster:Stop(0.000000001)
							else
								anims.holster:Stop(0.25)
							end

							if anims.holsterIdle then
								anims.holsterIdle:Stop(0.2)
							end
						end
					end

					if not AI then
						setGlobal("gunEquip", anims.equip.IsPlaying)
					end

					--[[
					if isRunning() then
						anims.holster:AdjustSpeed(linearVelocity.magnitude / 10)
					else
						anims.holster:AdjustSpeed(0)
					end]]

					if getGlobal("aim") and (AI or _G.isFPS()) and anims.idle.IsPlaying then
						anims.idle:Stop(0.000000001)
					end

					if anims.inspect then
						if anims.inspect.IsPlaying then
							setGlobal("aim", false)
						end
					end

					if anims.inspect_empty then
						if anims.inspect_empty.IsPlaying then
							setGlobal("aim", false)
						end
					end

					if not AI then
						setGlobal("inspect", anims.inspect and anims.inspect.IsPlaying or anims.inspect_empty and anims.inspect_empty.IsPlaying)
					end

					if anims.holster and anims.holster.IsPlaying then
						anims.idle:Stop(0.25)
					elseif not anims.idle.IsPlaying then
						anims.idle:Play(getGlobal("aim") and (AI or _G.isFPS()) and 0.000000001 or nil)
					end

					if anims.holsterIdle and not getGlobal("run") and not getGlobal("reload") then
						anims.holsterIdle:AdjustSpeed(0)
					elseif getGlobal("run") or getGlobal("reload") then
						if anims.holsterIdle then
							anims.holsterIdle:Stop(0.25)
						end
					end

					if not AI and handle:FindFirstChild("MuzzleFX") then
						local blocked, blockedPos = sightBlocked(handle.MuzzleFX)
						if blocked and humanoid.Health > 0 and not humanoid:GetAttribute("Downed") then
							UI.cantShoot.Visible = true
							local pos,_ = camera:WorldToScreenPoint(blockedPos)
							UI.cantShoot.Position = UDim2.new(0, pos.x, 0, pos.y + 36)
						else
							UI.cantShoot.Visible = false
						end

						if _G.isFPS() then
							Storage.Events.client:Fire("crosshair", not _G.aim, settings.crosshairRadius)
						else
							Storage.Events.client:Fire("crosshair", true, settings.crosshairRadius * (_G.aim and 0.5 or 1))
							if aimCrosshair and not _G.aim then
								aimCrosshair = false
								Storage.Events.client:Fire("setCrosshair", settings.crosshairRadius, true, nil, nil, 0.25)
							elseif not aimCrosshair and _G.aim then
								aimCrosshair = true
								Storage.Events.client:Fire("setCrosshair", settings.crosshairRadius * 0.5, true, nil, nil, 0.25)
							end
						end

						gunInfo.gunName.Text = tostring(tool.Name) .. " []"
						if isEquipped() and data then
							gunInfo.currentAmmo.Text = data.magCurrent
							gunInfo.magSize.Text = data.ammo
							gunInfo.fireMode.Text = (fireMode == 1 and "[SINGLE]") or (fireMode == 2 and "[BURST]") or (fireMode == 3 and "[AUTO]")
							gunInfo.currentAmmo.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 0, 0):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), (data.magCurrent / data.magSize) * 1)
							gunInfo.magSize.TextColor3 = data.ammo > 0 and Color3.fromRGB(229, 229, 229) or Color3.fromRGB(79, 0, 0)
						else
							gunInfo.currentAmmo.Text = "-"
							gunInfo.magSize.Text = "-"
						end

						if tool._mod.Handle:FindFirstChild("aimPos") then
							local dist = getDistance(tool._mod.Handle.aimPos.WorldPosition, camera.CFrame.Position)
							if _G.aim and _G.isFPS() then
								if dist < 0.35 then
									if not isActuallyAiming and settings.sniper and not AI then
										_G.createSound(player.PlayerGui.SOUNDS.zoom, player.PlayerGui):Play()
									end
									isActuallyAiming = true
								end
								if isActuallyAiming then
									if data.magCurrent > 0 then
										anims.fire:AdjustWeight(settings.aimWeight)
									end
									TweenService:Create(camera, settings.ads_config.tweenInfoIn, {FieldOfView = settings.ads_config.fovIn}):Play()
								end
							elseif not _G.aim or not _G.isFPS() then
								isActuallyAiming = false
								TweenService:Create(camera, settings.ads_config.tweenInfoOut, {FieldOfView = settings.ads_config.fovOut}):Play()
								_G.aimTickTimer = settings.ads_config.aimOutTime and settings.ads_config.aimOutTime + 0.1 or 0.35
							end
						end
					end
				end
			elseif not tool:IsDescendantOf(character) and not AI then
				TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(16e-5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {FieldOfView = 70}):Play()
			end
		end))
	end
	if tool:IsDescendantOf(character) then
		onEquipped()
	end

	tool.Equipped:Connect(onEquipped)
	tool.Unequipped:Connect(function()
		if not AI then
			UI.cantShoot.Visible = false
		end
		active = false

		reloading = false
		_G.gunEquipped = false
		_G.inspect = false

		for i,v in pairs(connections) do
			v:Disconnect()
			connections[i] = nil
		end

		--if not AI then
		--for index,frame in pairs(keyHints) do
		--if frame:IsDescendantOf(game) then
		--_G.clearKeyHint(frame)
		--end

		--keyHints[index] = nil
		--end
		--keyHints = {}

		--TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(16e-5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine), {FieldOfView = 70}):Play()
		--end

		for _,v in pairs(anims) do
			v:AdjustWeight(0)
			v:Stop()
		end
	end)

	local connection
	connection = tool.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
		if parent == nil then
			connection:Disconnect()

			for index,v in pairs(anims) do
				if typeof(anims[index]) == "table" then
					for _,k in pairs(anims[index]) do
						k:AdjustWeight(0)
						k:Stop()
					end
				else
					v:AdjustWeight(0)
					v:Stop()
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end  -  Editar

  03:10:35.982  return function(script)
	local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")

	local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
	local StorageServer = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

	local tool = script.Parent
	local player = tool.Parent.Parent

	local settings = require(Storage.Modules.Weapons:WaitForChild(tool.Name))

	local character = player:IsA("Player") and (player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()) or tool.Parent
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

	tool:SetAttribute("BulletHit", 0)
	tool:SetAttribute("LastAmmo", settings.magCurrent)
	tool.Motor.Part0 = settings.leftArmMotor and character:WaitForChild("Left Arm") or character:WaitForChild("Right Arm")
	for _,v in pairs(tool._data:GetChildren()) do
		if settings[v.Name] then
			v.Value = settings[v.Name]
		end
	end

	if player:IsA("Player") then
		local connection
		connection = tool.Equipped:Connect(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
			local ff = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField")
			if ff and ff.Name == "SpawnFF" then
				ff:Destroy()
			end
		end)

		local clientFramework = StorageServer.Modules.Frameworks.Guns.Client:Clone()
		clientFramework.Name = tool.Name.. "-client_framework"
		clientFramework.Parent = tool
		task.wait(5)
		clientFramework:Destroy()
	else
		require(StorageServer.Modules.Frameworks.Guns.Client)(script, character)
	end
end  -  Editar

  03:10:35.984  PATH: ServerStorage.Storage.Modules.CharacterEffects  -  Editar
  03:10:35.984  TYPE: ModuleScript  -  Editar
  03:10:35.984  ------------------------------------------  -  Editar
  03:10:35.984  local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

local CharacterEffects = require(Storage.Modules.CharacterEffects)

local module = {}
function module:createEffects(humanoid)
	for name,data in pairs(CharacterEffects) do
		humanoid:SetAttribute(name, false)
		for attributeName,attributeValue in pairs(data.attributes) do
			humanoid:SetAttribute(attributeName, attributeValue)
		end
		local particles = {}
		particles.burning = {}
		
		humanoid:GetAttributeChangedSignal(name):Connect(function()
			local state = humanoid:GetAttribute(name)
			if state then
				if name == "Burning" then
					for _,v in pairs(humanoid.Parent:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
							for _,p in pairs(Storage.Particles.BurningEffect:GetChildren()) do
								local n = p:Clone()
								n.Enabled = true
								n.Parent = v
								particles.burning[#particles.burning + 1] = n
							end
						end
					end
				end
			else
				if name == "Burning" then
					for i,v in pairs(particles.burning) do
						v.Enabled = false
						Debris:AddItem(v, 3)
						particles.burning[i] = nil
					end
					
					humanoid:SetAttribute("BurnCount", 0)
				end
			end
		end)
		
		local connection
		connection = humanoid.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
			if parent == nil then
				connection:Disconnect()
				table.clear(particles)
			end
		end)
	end
end

return module  -  Editar
  03:10:35.984

  03:10:35.985  ------------------------------------------  -  Editar
  03:10:35.985  local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
local StorageServer = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

local Network = require(Storage.Modules.Network)
local WorldToGui = require(Storage.Modules.WorldToGui)
local CombatTag = require(Storage.Modules.CombatTag)

local plane = script.Parent

task.wait(1 / 30)
local main = plane:WaitForChild("Main")
local clone = plane:Clone()

local drivepower = math.random(1300, 1700)
local drivedamping = math.random(650, 950)
local cruiseheight = math.random(450, 650)

local moveForce = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
moveForce.Parent = main

local yawForce = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
yawForce.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e4, 1e4, 1e4)
yawForce.D = drivedamping
yawForce.P = drivepower
yawForce.Parent = main

local lastPos = main.Position
local lastYaw = Vector3.new()
local maxParts = #plane.Body:GetChildren()

local crashed = false

local config = {
	thruster_force = 450;
	process_rate = 1 / 15;
	minigun_firerate = 0.04;
	minigun_range = 2000;
	minigun_damage = 200;
}

function lerp(a, b, t)
	return a + (b - a) * t
end

function getDistance(p1, p2)
	return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
end

function createSound(sound, parent)
	local newSound = sound:Clone()
	if typeof(parent) == "Vector3" then
		local a = Instance.new("Attachment")
		a.Parent = workspace.Terrain
		a.WorldPosition = parent
		newSound.Parent = a
		Debris:AddItem(a, newSound.TimeLength + 1)
	else
		newSound.Parent = parent
		Debris:AddItem(newSound, newSound.TimeLength + 1)
	end

	return newSound
end

function getRandomChild(parent)
	return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
end

local function createGuiSurface(texture, part, pos, scale, size, config)
	local surfaceFace, width, height, relativeX, relativeY = WorldToGui:WorldPositionToGuiPosition(part, pos)
	
	if surfaceFace and width and height and relativeX and relativeY then
		local surfaceGui = Instance.new("SurfaceGui")
		surfaceGui.CanvasSize = Vector2.new(scale * width, scale * height)
		surfaceGui.LightInfluence = 1
		surfaceGui.Face = surfaceFace

		local surfaceFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
		surfaceFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		surfaceFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		surfaceFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
		surfaceFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		surfaceFrame.Parent = surfaceGui

		local decal = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		decal.Image = texture
		decal.BorderSizePixel = 0
		decal.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		decal.Size = UDim2.new(0, scale * size.x, 0, scale * size.y)
		decal.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		decal.Position = UDim2.new(relativeX, 0, relativeY, 0)
		for prop,data in pairs(config) do
			decal[prop] = data
		end
		decal.Parent = surfaceFrame

		surfaceGui.Parent = part
		Debris:AddItem(surfaceGui, 10)

		return surfaceGui
	end
	
	return false
end

function isHumanoid(part)
	if part and part.Parent and part.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		return part.Parent
	elseif part and part.Parent and part.Parent ~= workspace then
		return isHumanoid(part.Parent)
	end

	return false
end

function getWeld(part)
	local weld
	for _,v in pairs(plane.Welds:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Part1 == part then
			weld = v
			break
		end
	end
	
	return weld
end

function getAvailableParts()
	local n = 0
	for _,v in pairs(plane.Welds:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Enabled then
			n += 1
		end
	end
	
	return n
end

function burn(part)
	for _,p in pairs(main.fireFX:GetChildren()) do
		local v = p:Clone()
		v.Parent = part
		v.Enabled = true
	end
end

function moveTo(position)
	local dir = (position - main.Position).unit
	local moveDirection = ((main.Position + main.Velocity) - main.Position).unit
	local yawAngle = main.CFrame:vectorToWorldSpace(moveDirection)
	local yawX = (lastYaw - yawAngle).x * 250
	local pos = main.Position + dir
	local yaw = CFrame.new(pos, pos + dir) * CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(yawX))
	yawForce.CFrame = yaw
	
	lastYaw = yawAngle
end

function inFOV(part, fov)
	local directionVector = (part.Position - main.Position).unit
	local forwardVector = main.CFrame.lookVector
	
	local dotProduct = directionVector:Dot(forwardVector)
	
	local halfFovAngle = math.rad(fov / 2)
	local cosHalfFovAngle = math.cos(halfFovAngle)
	return dotProduct >= cosHalfFovAngle
end

local function emitParticle(name, multiplier, v, dur)
	local n = Storage.Particles[name].Attachment:Clone()
	if typeof(v) == "Vector3" then
		n.Parent = workspace.Terrain
		n.WorldPosition = v
	else
		n.Parent = v
	end
	for _,p in pairs(n:GetChildren()) do
		p:Emit(p:GetAttribute("EmitCount") * (multiplier or 1))
	end
	Debris:AddItem(n, dur, 3)

	return n
end

local aimDirection = Vector3.new()
local fire = false
local ammo = 150
task.spawn(function()
	local minigun = plane.Body:WaitForChild("Minigun")
	while not crashed and ammo > 0 do
		if fire then
			for _,v in pairs(minigun.fx:GetChildren()) do
				v.Enabled = true
			end

			minigun.fire:Play()
			--createSound(getRandomChild(minigun.stops), minigun):Play()
			local accuracy = 10
			while fire and not crashed and ammo > 0 do
				ammo -= 1
				task.spawn(function()
					local bullet = Storage.Miscs.A10_bullet:Clone()
					bullet.CFrame = minigun.fx.WorldCFrame
					bullet.Parent = workspace.Terrain
					
					local params = RaycastParams.new()
					params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {plane}
					local origin = minigun.fx.WorldPosition
					local dir = (aimDirection - origin).unit * config.minigun_range
						+ Vector3.new(
							math.random(-10000, 10000) / 100,
							math.random(-10000, 10000) / 100,
							math.random(-10000, 10000) / 100
						)
						+ main.Velocity / accuracy
					accuracy = math.max(accuracy - 0.25, 1)
					local cast = workspace:Raycast(origin, dir, params)
					if cast then
						local params = RaycastParams.new()
						params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
						params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist
						local crater = workspace:Raycast(cast.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0), Vector3.new(0, -15, 0), params)
						if crater then
							local size = math.random(1500, 2000) / 100
							createGuiSurface(
								"rbxassetid://2828069358",
								crater.Instance,
								crater.Position,
								128,
								Vector2.new(size, size),
								{
									["ImageTransparency"] = math.random(450, 750) / 1000,
									["Rotation"] = 0,
									["ResampleMode"] = Enum.ResamplerMode.Pixelated,
								}
							)
						end
						
						for _,player in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
							local pChar = player.Character
							local pHum = pChar:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
							if pHum.Health > 0 and pHum.RootPart and getDistance(pHum.RootPart, cast.Position) <= 250 then
								local dist = getDistance(pHum.RootPart, cast.Position)
								Storage.Events.shake:FireClient(player, {Damping = 1.2}, math.clamp(150 - dist, 20, 120), 1.5, true)
							end
						end
						
						local explosion = Instance.new("Explosion")
						explosion.BlastPressure = 0
						explosion.BlastRadius = 30
						explosion.ExplosionType = Enum.ExplosionType.NoCraters
						explosion.DestroyJointRadiusPercent = 0
						explosion.Position = cast.Position
						explosion.Parent = workspace.Terrain
						
						local targets = {}
						local params = OverlapParams.new()
						params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {plane}
						local collideParts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(cast.Position, 30, params)
						for _,part in pairs(collideParts) do
							local target = isHumanoid(part)
							local player = target and game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)
							local pvp = player and ReplicatedStorage.__profiles[player.Name].pvp.Value or not player
							if target and not targets[target] and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Characters) and not target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ForceField") and pvp and (not player or player ~= plane.user.Value) then
								targets[target] = true
								
								task.defer(function()
									local parts = {}
									if target.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
										for _,part in pairs(target:GetChildren()) do
											if part:IsA("BasePart") then
												parts[#parts + 1] = part

												if math.random(5) == 1 then
													break
												end
											end
										end
									end
									
									if Players:FindFirstChild(plane.killer.Value) then
										CombatTag:addTag(target, Players[plane.killer.Value].Character, {damage = config.minigun_damage, weapon = plane.Name})
									end
									target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(config.minigun_damage)
									target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("RagdollDur", 3.5)
									target.Humanoid:SetAttribute("Ragdoll", true)

									local force = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
									force.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e11, 1e11, 1e11)
									force.Velocity = (target.Humanoid.RootPart.Position - cast.Position).unit * math.random(30, 40) + Vector3.new(0, math.random(30, 40), 0)
									force.Parent = part
									Debris:AddItem(force, 0.1)
									part.RotVelocity = part.Velocity * 2

									task.defer(function()
										for _,part in pairs(parts) do
											if part:GetAttribute("Limbs") then
												part:SetAttribute("Enabled", false)
												_G.createSound(getRandomChild(Storage.Sounds.fleshHits), part):Play()
												emitParticle("BloodHitEffect", nil, part.Position)
											end
										end
									end)
								end)
							elseif target and target:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Objects) then
								task.wait(1 / 15)
								if target:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
									if Players:FindFirstChild(plane.killer.Value) then
										CombatTag:addTag(target, Players[plane.killer.Value].Character, {damage = 1000, weapon = plane.Name})
									end
									target.Humanoid:TakeDamage(1000)
								end
							elseif not target and part:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Buildings.Glass) then
								Storage.Events.glass:Fire(part, part.Position, (part.Position - cast.Position).unit * 35 + Vector3.new(0, math.random(8, 12), 0))
							end
						end
						
						local connection
						connection = explosion.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
							if parent == nil then
								connection:Disconnect()
								table.clear(targets)
							end
						end)
						
						bullet.explode:Play()
					end
					
					TweenService:Create(bullet, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {CFrame = cast and CFrame.new(cast.Position) or CFrame.new(origin + dir)}):Play()
					
					Debris:AddItem(bullet, 5)
				end)
				
				task.wait(config.minigun_firerate)
			end

			minigun.fire:Stop()
			createSound(getRandomChild(minigun.stops), minigun):Play()
			for _,v in pairs(minigun.fx:GetChildren()) do
				v.Enabled = false
			end
		end
		
		task.wait(1 / 15)
	end
end)

local jetTrails = {}
function jetTrail(enable)
	for _,v in pairs(jetTrails) do
		v.Enabled = enable
	end
end

local hitboxes = {}
for _,part in pairs(plane.Body:GetChildren()) do
	local index = #hitboxes + 1
	hitboxes[index] = part.Touched:Connect(function(hit)
		if not hit:IsDescendantOf(plane) and not hit:IsDescendantOf(workspace.Terrain) then
			local force = part.Velocity.magnitude
			if force > 250 then
				local weld = getWeld(part)
				if weld then weld.Enabled = false end
				local grav = Instance.new("BodyForce")
				grav.Force = Vector3.new(0, part:GetMass() * -workspace.Gravity, 0)
				grav.Parent = part
				part.Velocity /= 2
				part.RotVelocity = Vector3.new()
				part.CanCollide = true
				moveForce.MaxForce /= 2
				hitboxes[index]:Disconnect()
				hitboxes[index] = nil
				
				local crash = createSound(getRandomChild(main.crashes), part)
				crash.PlaybackSpeed += math.random() * 0.6
				crash:Play()
				burn(part)
				
				local explosion = Instance.new("Explosion")
				explosion.BlastPressure = 0
				explosion.BlastRadius = 0
				explosion.ExplosionType = Enum.ExplosionType.NoCraters
				explosion.DestroyJointRadiusPercent = 0
				explosion.Position = part.Position
				explosion.Parent = workspace.Terrain
				
				local trail = part:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Trail")
				if trail then trail.Enabled = false end
				
				if getAvailableParts() < maxParts - 5 then
					crashed = true

					moveForce:Destroy()
					yawForce:Destroy()

					--[[for i,_ in pairs(hitboxes) do
						hitboxes[i]:Disconnect()
						hitboxes[i] = nil
					end
					hitboxes = nil]]
				end
			end
		end
	end)
end

for _,v in pairs(plane.Body:GetDescendants()) do
	if v:IsA("Trail") and v.Name == "JetTrail" then
		--v.Enabled = true
		jetTrails[#jetTrails + 1] = v
	elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") and v.Name == "Thruster" then
		v.Enabled = true
	end
end
local engineVolume = main.engine.Volume
main.engine.Volume = 0
main.engine:Play()
TweenService:Create(main.engine, TweenInfo.new(5), {Volume = engineVolume}):Play()
main.scream:Play()
main.strafe:Play()

--local target = workspace:WaitForChild("zoneqyx"):WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
main:SetNetworkOwner(nil)
local basePos = main.Position
while not crashed or #plane.Body:GetChildren() < 1 or not main:IsDescendantOf(workspace) do
	local dist = getDistance(plane.pos.Value, main)
	local heightFromPlane = main.Position.y - plane.pos.Value.y
	local currentHeight = Vector3.new(0, cruiseheight, 0)
	local lower = (plane.pos.Value - main.Position).magnitude < (plane.pos.Value - lastPos).magnitude
	local speed = config.thruster_force
	local targetPos = plane.pos.Value
	
	if ammo > 0 then
		if heightFromPlane < cruiseheight * 0.45 then
			--print("Ascending")
			yawForce.P = drivepower
			yawForce.D = drivedamping * 0.75
			currentHeight = Vector3.new(0, cruiseheight * 2, 0)
			--targetPos = main.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 500, 0)
		elseif heightFromPlane > cruiseheight * 0.75 then
			--print("Descending", heightFromPlane, cruiseheight * 0.75)
			yawForce.P = drivepower
			yawForce.D = drivedamping
			currentHeight = Vector3.new(0, -cruiseheight, 0)
		end

		if heightFromPlane < cruiseheight * 1.2 and dist < 500 and lower then
			speed *= 0.75
			--print("Attacking")
			--[[yawForce.P = drivepower
			yawForce.D = drivedamping * 1.2
			currentHeight = Vector3.new(0, cruiseheight, 0)]]

			aimDirection = plane.pos.Value
			fire = true
		elseif dist > 650 then
			fire = false
		end

		if heightFromPlane < 180 then
			--print("Too low")
			yawForce.P = drivepower
			yawForce.D = drivedamping * 0.35
			targetPos = main.CFrame.Position + Vector3.new(0, 500, 0)
		end

		if dist > 500 and heightFromPlane < 50 then
			yawForce.D *= 1.2
			currentHeight *= 5
		end
	else
		yawForce.P = drivepower
		yawForce.D = drivedamping * 0.5
		targetPos = basePos * 100 + Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0)
		Debris:AddItem(plane, 20)
		TweenService:Create(main.engine, TweenInfo.new(10), {Volume = 0}):Play()
		TweenService:Create(main.strafe, TweenInfo.new(15), {Volume = 0}):Play()
	end
	
	jetTrail(not lower)
	
	moveTo(targetPos + currentHeight)
	moveForce.Velocity = main.CFrame.LookVector * speed
	
	lastPos = main.Position
	task.wait(config.process_rate)
end

for _,v in pairs(plane:GetDescendants()) do
	if v:IsA("Sound") then
		v:Stop()
	elseif v:IsA("ParticleEmitter") and v.Name == "Thruster" then
		v.Enabled = false
	end
end
main.crash:Play()

for _,v in pairs(plane.Body:GetChildren()) do
	if not v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BodyForce") then
		local grav = Instance.new("BodyForce")
		grav.Force = Vector3.new(0, v:GetMass() * -workspace.Gravity, 0)
		grav.Parent = v
		v.Velocity /= 5
		v.RotVelocity /= 5
	end
	
	local trail = v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Trail")
	if trail then trail.Enabled = false end
	
	v.CanCollide = true
end

task.wait(8)
table.clear(hitboxes)
clone.Parent = plane.Parent
plane:Destroy()  -  Editar

  03:10:36.019  local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

local Network = require(Storage.Modules.Network)

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse = player:GetMouse()
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

local character = player.Character
if not character then
	character = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
else
	task.wait(1 / 30)
end
character = player.Character

local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local tool = script.Parent

local UI = player.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("UI")

local function loadAnim(id)
	local animTrack = Instance.new("Animation")
	animTrack.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
	return animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack),
		animTrack:Destroy()
end


local anims = {
	["idle"] = loadAnim(13794363918),
	["call"] = loadAnim(13794367048)
}

local used = false

local airstrikeLocation = Storage.Miscs.airstrikeLocation:Clone()
local cameraTween

local params = RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist

local function mouseHit()
	local unitRay = mouse.UnitRay
	local cast = workspace:Raycast(unitRay.Origin, unitRay.Direction.unit * 1000, params)
	return cast
end

local function getDistance(p1, p2)
	return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
end

local function getRandomChild(parent)
	return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
end

local equipTick = 0

local function isEquipped()
	return tick() - equipTick >= 0.8
end

local connections = {}
tool.Equipped:Connect(function()
	equipTick = tick()

	anims.idle:Play()
	pcall(function() airstrikeLocation.Parent = workspace.Ignored end)
	
	if not used then
		local hit = mouseHit()
		if hit then
			airstrikeLocation.CFrame = CFrame.new(hit.Position)
		end

		tool:SetAttribute("NoCameraBobbing", true)
		camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
	end
	
	table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local hit = mouseHit()
		if hit and not used then
			TweenService:Create(airstrikeLocation, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {CFrame = CFrame.new(hit.Position)}):Play()
		end
		
		if humanoid.RootPart and not used then
			cameraTween = TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(1), {CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoid.RootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 100, 0), airstrikeLocation.Position)})
			cameraTween:Play()
		end
	end))

	table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
		if not process then
			if inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and humanoid.Health > 0 and isEquipped() and not used and mouse.Hit then
				used = true
				tool.activated.Value = true
				
				TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(1), {CFrame = humanoid.RootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)}):Play()
				tool:SetAttribute("NoCameraBobbing", true)
				
				anims.call:Play()
				tool.__event:FireServer(airstrikeLocation.CFrame)
				Debris:AddItem(airstrikeLocation, 3)
				
				task.wait(1)
				camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
				camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
			end
		end
	end))
end)

tool.Unequipped:Connect(function()
	if not used then
		airstrikeLocation.Parent = nil
	end
	
	tool:SetAttribute("NoCameraBobbing", false)
	camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
	camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
	
	if cameraTween then
		cameraTween:Cancel()
	end
	
	for i,v in pairs(connections) do
		v:Disconnect()
		connections[i] = nil
	end

	for _,v in pairs(anims) do
		v:AdjustWeight(0)
		v:Stop()
	end
end)  -  Editar

  03:10:36.019  local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local ServerStorage = game:GetService("ServerStorage")
local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

local StorageServer = ServerStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")
local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

local tool = script.Parent
local player = tool.Parent.Parent

local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

tool.Motor.Part0 = character:WaitForChild("Right Arm")

local function clampDistanceBetweenVectors(V1, V2, Max) -- V1 is origin.
	return V1 + ((V2 - V1).Unit * math.min((V2 - V1).Magnitude,  Max))
end

tool:WaitForChild("__event").OnServerEvent:Connect(function(client, cf)
	if client == player and typeof(cf) == "CFrame" and tool.activated.Value == false then
		tool.activated.Value = true
		tool._mod.Handle.Sound:Play()
		
		task.wait(tool._mod.Handle.Sound.TimeLength + 0.5)
		tool.Parent = workspace.Ignored
		tool.Motor.Part0 = nil
		tool._mod.Handle.CollisionGroup = "Debris"
		tool._mod.Handle.CanCollide = true
		
		local a10 = StorageServer.Models["A10 Warthog"]:Clone()
		a10.killer.Value = player.Name
		a10.user.Value = player
		a10.pos.Value = cf.Position
		local originCF = a10.Main.CFrame
		local targetPosV3 = cf.Position
		local spawnPos = Vector3.new(targetPosV3.x + math.random(-750, 750), originCF.Position.y, targetPosV3.z + math.random(-750, 750))
		spawnPos = clampDistanceBetweenVectors(targetPosV3, spawnPos, 500)
		a10.Main.CFrame = CFrame.new(spawnPos, Vector3.new(targetPosV3.x, originCF.Position.y, targetPosV3.z))
		a10.Parent = workspace.Ignored
		
		Debris:AddItem(tool, 5)
	end
end)  -  Editar

				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=182435998", weight = 10 }, 
				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=182491037", weight = 10 }, 
				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=182491065", weight = 10 } 
			},
	dance2 = {
				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=182436842", weight = 10 }, 
				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=182491248", weight = 10 }, 
				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=182491277", weight = 10 } 
			},
	dance3 = {
				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=182436935", weight = 10 }, 
				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=182491368", weight = 10 }, 
				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=182491423", weight = 10 } 
			},
	laugh = {
				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=129423131", weight = 10 } 
			},
	cheer = {
				{ id = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=129423030", weight = 10 } 
			},

local dances = {"dance1", "dance2", "dance3"}

-- Existance in this list signifies that it is an emote, the value indicates if it is a looping emote
local emoteNames = { wave = false, point = false, dance1 = true, dance2 = true, dance3 = true, laugh = false, cheer = false}

function configureAnimationSet(name, fileList)
	if (animTable[name] ~= nil) then
		for _, connection in pairs(animTable[name].connections) do
			connection:disconnect()
		end
	end
	animTable[name] = {}
	animTable[name].count = 0
	animTable[name].totalWeight = 0	
	animTable[name].connections = {}

	-- check for config values
	local config = script:FindFirstChild(name)
	if (config ~= nil) then
--		print("Loading anims " .. name)
		table.insert(animTable[name].connections, config.ChildAdded:connect(function(child) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
		table.insert(animTable[name].connections, config.ChildRemoved:connect(function(child) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
		local idx = 1
		for _, childPart in pairs(config:GetChildren()) do
			if (childPart:IsA("Animation")) then
				table.insert(animTable[name].connections, childPart.Changed:connect(function(property) configureAnimationSet(name, fileList) end))
				animTable[name][idx] = {}
				animTable[name][idx].anim = childPart
				local weightObject = childPart:FindFirstChild("Weight")
				if (weightObject == nil) then
					animTable[name][idx].weight = 1
				else
					animTable[name][idx].weight = weightObject.Value
				end
				animTable[name].count = animTable[name].count + 1
				animTable[name].totalWeight = animTable[name].totalWeight + animTable[name][idx].weight
	--			print(name .. " [" .. idx .. "] " .. animTable[name][idx].anim.AnimationId .. " (" .. animTable[name][idx].weight .. ")")
				idx = idx + 1
			end
		end
	end

	-- fallback to defaults
	if (animTable[name].count <= 0) then
		for idx, anim in pairs(fileList) do
			animTable[name][idx] = {}
			animTable[name][idx].anim = Instance.new("Animation")
			animTable[name][idx].anim.Name = name
			animTable[name][idx].anim.AnimationId = anim.id
			animTable[name][idx].weight = anim.weight
			animTable[name].count = animTable[name].count + 1
			animTable[name].totalWeight = animTable[name].totalWeight + anim.weight
--			print(name .. " [" .. idx .. "] " .. anim.id .. " (" .. anim.weight .. ")")
		end
	end
end

-- Setup animation objects
function scriptChildModified(child)
	local fileList = animNames[child.Name]
	if (fileList ~= nil) then
		configureAnimationSet(child.Name, fileList)
	end	
end

script.ChildAdded:connect(scriptChildModified)
script.ChildRemoved:connect(scriptChildModified)

-- Clear any existing animation tracks
-- Fixes issue with characters that are moved in and out of the Workspace accumulating tracks
local animator = if Humanoid then Humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") else nil
if animator then
	local animTracks = animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()
	for i,track in ipairs(animTracks) do
		track:Stop(0)
		track:Destroy()
	end
end


for name, fileList in pairs(animNames) do 
	configureAnimationSet(name, fileList)
end	

-- ANIMATION

-- declarations
local toolAnim = "None"
local toolAnimTime = 0

local jumpAnimTime = 0
local jumpAnimDuration = 0.3

local toolTransitionTime = 0.1
local fallTransitionTime = 0.3
local jumpMaxLimbVelocity = 0.75

-- functions

function stopAllAnimations()
	local oldAnim = currentAnim

	-- return to idle if finishing an emote
	if (emoteNames[oldAnim] ~= nil and emoteNames[oldAnim] == false) then
		oldAnim = "idle"
	end

	currentAnim = ""
	currentAnimInstance = nil
	if (currentAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
		currentAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
	end

	if (currentAnimTrack ~= nil) then
		currentAnimTrack:Stop()
		currentAnimTrack:Destroy()
		currentAnimTrack = nil
	end
	return oldAnim
end

function setAnimationSpeed(speed)
	if speed ~= currentAnimSpeed then
		currentAnimSpeed = speed
		currentAnimTrack:AdjustSpeed(currentAnimSpeed)
	end
end

function keyFrameReachedFunc(frameName)
	if (frameName == "End") then

		local repeatAnim = currentAnim
		-- return to idle if finishing an emote
		if (emoteNames[repeatAnim] ~= nil and emoteNames[repeatAnim] == false) then
			repeatAnim = "idle"
		end
		
		local animSpeed = currentAnimSpeed
		playAnimation(repeatAnim, 0.0, Humanoid)
		setAnimationSpeed(animSpeed)
	end
end

-- Preload animations
function playAnimation(animName, transitionTime, humanoid) 
		
	local roll = math.random(1, animTable[animName].totalWeight) 
	local origRoll = roll
	local idx = 1
	while (roll > animTable[animName][idx].weight) do
		roll = roll - animTable[animName][idx].weight
		idx = idx + 1
	end
--		print(animName .. " " .. idx .. " [" .. origRoll .. "]")
	local anim = animTable[animName][idx].anim

	-- switch animation		
	if (anim ~= currentAnimInstance) then
		
		if (currentAnimTrack ~= nil) then
			currentAnimTrack:Stop(transitionTime)
			currentAnimTrack:Destroy()
		end

		currentAnimSpeed = 1.0
	
		-- load it to the humanoid; get AnimationTrack
		currentAnimTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim)
		currentAnimTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Core
		 
		-- play the animation
		currentAnimTrack:Play(transitionTime)
		currentAnim = animName
		currentAnimInstance = anim

		-- set up keyframe name triggers
		if (currentAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
			currentAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
		end
		currentAnimKeyframeHandler = currentAnimTrack.KeyframeReached:connect(keyFrameReachedFunc)
		
	end

end

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

local toolAnimName = ""
local toolAnimTrack = nil
local toolAnimInstance = nil
local currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler = nil

function toolKeyFrameReachedFunc(frameName)
	if (frameName == "End") then
--		print("Keyframe : ".. frameName)	
		playToolAnimation(toolAnimName, 0.0, Humanoid)
	end
end


function playToolAnimation(animName, transitionTime, humanoid, priority)	 
		
		local roll = math.random(1, animTable[animName].totalWeight) 
		local origRoll = roll
		local idx = 1
		while (roll > animTable[animName][idx].weight) do
			roll = roll - animTable[animName][idx].weight
			idx = idx + 1
		end
--		print(animName .. " * " .. idx .. " [" .. origRoll .. "]")
		local anim = animTable[animName][idx].anim

		if (toolAnimInstance ~= anim) then
			
			if (toolAnimTrack ~= nil) then
				toolAnimTrack:Stop()
				toolAnimTrack:Destroy()
				transitionTime = 0
			end
					
			-- load it to the humanoid; get AnimationTrack
			toolAnimTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim)
			if priority then
				toolAnimTrack.Priority = priority
			end
			 
			-- play the animation
			toolAnimTrack:Play(transitionTime)
			toolAnimName = animName
			toolAnimInstance = anim

			currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler = toolAnimTrack.KeyframeReached:connect(toolKeyFrameReachedFunc)
		end
end

function stopToolAnimations()
	local oldAnim = toolAnimName

	if (currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler ~= nil) then
		currentToolAnimKeyframeHandler:disconnect()
	end

	toolAnimName = ""
	toolAnimInstance = nil
	if (toolAnimTrack ~= nil) then
		toolAnimTrack:Stop()
		toolAnimTrack:Destroy()
		toolAnimTrack = nil
	end


	return oldAnim
end

-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


function onRunning(speed)
	if speed > 0.01 then
		playAnimation("walk", 0.1, Humanoid)
		if currentAnimInstance and currentAnimInstance.AnimationId == "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=180426354" then
			setAnimationSpeed(speed / 14.5)
		end
		pose = "Running"
	else
		if emoteNames[currentAnim] == nil then
			playAnimation("idle", 0.1, Humanoid)
			pose = "Standing"
		end
	end
end

function onDied()
	pose = "Dead"
end

function onJumping()
	playAnimation("jump", 0.1, Humanoid)
	jumpAnimTime = jumpAnimDuration
	pose = "Jumping"
end

function onClimbing(speed)
	playAnimation("climb", 0.1, Humanoid)
	setAnimationSpeed(speed / 12.0)
	pose = "Climbing"
end

function onGettingUp()
	pose = "GettingUp"
end

function onFreeFall()
	if (jumpAnimTime <= 0) then
		playAnimation("fall", fallTransitionTime, Humanoid)
	end
	pose = "FreeFall"
end

function onFallingDown()
	pose = "FallingDown"
end

function onSeated()
	pose = "Seated"
end

function onPlatformStanding()
	pose = "PlatformStanding"
end

function onSwimming(speed)
	if speed > 0 then
		pose = "Running"
	else
		pose = "Standing"
	end
end

function getTool()	
	for _, kid in ipairs(Figure:GetChildren()) do
		if kid.className == "Tool" then return kid end
	end
	return nil
end

function getToolAnim(tool)
	for _, c in ipairs(tool:GetChildren()) do
		if c.Name == "toolanim" and c.className == "StringValue" then
			return c
		end
	end
	return nil
end

function animateTool()
	
	if (toolAnim == "None") then
		playToolAnimation("toolnone", toolTransitionTime, Humanoid, Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle)
		return
	end

	if (toolAnim == "Slash") then
		playToolAnimation("toolslash", 0, Humanoid, Enum.AnimationPriority.Action)
		return
	end

	if (toolAnim == "Lunge") then
		playToolAnimation("toollunge", 0, Humanoid, Enum.AnimationPriority.Action)
		return
	end
end

function moveSit()
	RightShoulder.MaxVelocity = 0.15
	LeftShoulder.MaxVelocity = 0.15
	RightShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(3.14 /2)
	LeftShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(-3.14 /2)
	RightHip:SetDesiredAngle(3.14 /2)
	LeftHip:SetDesiredAngle(-3.14 /2)
end

local lastTick = 0

function move(time)
	local amplitude = 1
	local frequency = 1
  	local deltaTime = time - lastTick
  	lastTick = time

	local climbFudge = 0
	local setAngles = false

  	if (jumpAnimTime > 0) then
  		jumpAnimTime = jumpAnimTime - deltaTime
  	end

	if (pose == "FreeFall" and jumpAnimTime <= 0) then
		playAnimation("fall", fallTransitionTime, Humanoid)
	elseif (pose == "Seated") then
		playAnimation("sit", 0.5, Humanoid)
		return
	elseif (pose == "Running") then
		playAnimation("walk", 0.1, Humanoid)
	elseif (pose == "Dead" or pose == "GettingUp" or pose == "FallingDown" or pose == "Seated" or pose == "PlatformStanding") then
--		print("Wha " .. pose)
		stopAllAnimations()
		amplitude = 0.1
		frequency = 1
		setAngles = true
	end

	if (setAngles) then
		local desiredAngle = amplitude * math.sin(time * frequency)

		RightShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(desiredAngle + climbFudge)
		LeftShoulder:SetDesiredAngle(desiredAngle - climbFudge)
		RightHip:SetDesiredAngle(-desiredAngle)
		LeftHip:SetDesiredAngle(-desiredAngle)
	end

	-- Tool Animation handling
	local tool = getTool()
	if tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
	
		local animStringValueObject = getToolAnim(tool)

		if animStringValueObject then
			toolAnim = animStringValueObject.Value
			-- message recieved, delete StringValue
			animStringValueObject.Parent = nil
			toolAnimTime = time + .3
		end

		if time > toolAnimTime then
			toolAnimTime = 0
			toolAnim = "None"
		end

		animateTool()		
	else
		stopToolAnimations()
		toolAnim = "None"
		toolAnimInstance = nil
		toolAnimTime = 0
	end
end

-- connect events
Humanoid.Died:connect(onDied)
Humanoid.Running:connect(onRunning)
Humanoid.Jumping:connect(onJumping)
Humanoid.Climbing:connect(onClimbing)
Humanoid.GettingUp:connect(onGettingUp)
Humanoid.FreeFalling:connect(onFreeFall)
Humanoid.FallingDown:connect(onFallingDown)
Humanoid.Seated:connect(onSeated)
Humanoid.PlatformStanding:connect(onPlatformStanding)
Humanoid.Swimming:connect(onSwimming)

---- setup emote chat hook
game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Chatted:connect(function(msg)
	local emote = ""
	if msg == "/e dance" then
		emote = dances[math.random(1, #dances)]
	elseif (string.sub(msg, 1, 3) == "/e ") then
		emote = string.sub(msg, 4)
	elseif (string.sub(msg, 1, 7) == "/emote ") then
		emote = string.sub(msg, 8)
	end
	
	if (pose == "Standing" and emoteNames[emote] ~= nil) then
		playAnimation(emote, 0.1, Humanoid)
	end

end)

-- emote bindable hook
script:WaitForChild("PlayEmote").OnInvoke = function(emote)
	-- Only play emotes when idling
	if pose ~= "Standing" then
		return
	end
	if emoteNames[emote] ~= nil then
		-- Default emotes
		playAnimation(emote, EMOTE_TRANSITION_TIME, Humanoid)

		return true, currentAnimTrack
	end

	-- Return false to indicate that the emote could not be played
	return false
end
-- main program

-- initialize to idle
playAnimation("idle", 0.1, Humanoid)
pose = "Standing"

while Figure.Parent ~= nil do
	local _, time = wait(0.1)
	move(time)
end

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

local Storage = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Storage")

local Network = require(Storage.Modules.Network)

local player = Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse = player:GetMouse()
local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

local character = player.Character
if not character then
	character = player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
else
	task.wait(1 / 30)
end
character = player.Character

local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator")

local tool = script.Parent

local UI = player.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("UI")

local function loadAnim(id)
	local animTrack = Instance.new("Animation")
	animTrack.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
	return animator:LoadAnimation(animTrack),
		animTrack:Destroy()
end


local anims = {
	["idle"] = loadAnim(13794363918),
	["call"] = loadAnim(13794367048)
}

local used = false

local airstrikeLocation = Storage.Miscs.airstrikeLocation:Clone()
local cameraTween

local params = RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {workspace.Buildings}
params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Whitelist

local function mouseHit()
	local unitRay = mouse.UnitRay
	local cast = workspace:Raycast(unitRay.Origin, unitRay.Direction.unit * 1000, params)
	return cast
end

local function getDistance(p1, p2)
	return ((typeof(p1) == "Instance" and p1.Position or p1) - (typeof(p2) == "Instance" and p2.Position or p2)).magnitude
end

local function getRandomChild(parent)
	return parent:GetChildren()[math.random(#parent:GetChildren())]
end

local equipTick = 0

local function isEquipped()
	return tick() - equipTick >= 0.8
end

local connections = {}
tool.Equipped:Connect(function()
	equipTick = tick()

	anims.idle:Play()
	pcall(function() airstrikeLocation.Parent = workspace.Ignored end)
	
	if not used then
		local hit = mouseHit()
		if hit then
			airstrikeLocation.CFrame = CFrame.new(hit.Position)
		end

		tool:SetAttribute("NoCameraBobbing", true)
		camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
	end
	
	table.insert(connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
		local hit = mouseHit()
		if hit and not used then
			TweenService:Create(airstrikeLocation, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {CFrame = CFrame.new(hit.Position)}):Play()
		end
		
		if humanoid.RootPart and not used then
			cameraTween = TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(1), {CFrame = CFrame.new(humanoid.RootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 100, 0), airstrikeLocation.Position)})
			cameraTween:Play()
		end
	end))

	table.insert(connections, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject, process)
		if not process then
			if inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 and humanoid.Health > 0 and isEquipped() and not used and mouse.Hit then
				used = true
				tool.activated.Value = true
				
				TweenService:Create(camera, TweenInfo.new(1), {CFrame = humanoid.RootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)}):Play()
				tool:SetAttribute("NoCameraBobbing", true)
				
				anims.call:Play()
				tool.__event:FireServer(airstrikeLocation.CFrame)
				Debris:AddItem(airstrikeLocation, 3)
				
				task.wait(1)
				camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
				camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
			end
		end
	end))
end)

tool.Unequipped:Connect(function()
	if not used then
		airstrikeLocation.Parent = nil
	end
	
	tool:SetAttribute("NoCameraBobbing", false)
	camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
	camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
	
	if cameraTween then
		cameraTween:Cancel()
	end
	
	for i,v in pairs(connections) do
		v:Disconnect()
		connections[i] = nil
	end

	for _,v in pairs(anims) do
		v:AdjustWeight(0)
		v:Stop()
	end
end)  -  Editar
